<sub>⏱️ 2s</sub>

### 👤 User

JIRA Issue: DOC-3141

Title: Tag attribute editor validation

Description:
Many tag definitions in the repository are missing editor-level vscode validation (via decorators, etc.)

go through all tag definition and their attributes and add missing checks. Be as general and reusable with the implementation as possible - unless its some incredibly niche and custom thing like the codeLink tag, we dont want a custom decorater/provider for every little thing. A reusable approach like the one with required attributes is a perefect example of good code. 

In the handoff summary - thoroughly note your decisions and chosen approaches. 

Notes

  * You can consult the vscode api documentation for latest API.
  * Dont forget to run tests after
  * Write new tests for all added functionality
  * update copilot-instructions if some general/global codebase-level change was applied
  * make sure to update changelog, bump packge.json version by patch version
  * and commit the built vsix binary (attach it to this Jira issue as well)

Priority: Not set

Was this page helpful?: Helpful

JIRA Comments (oldest first):
[2026-02-14T16:45:22.383+0100] Jakub Skurek:
💪 Ralph here, reporting for validation duty! Time to make these tags behave. I have analyzed all 30+ tag definitions and identified several gaps in editor-level validation. Rolling up my sleeves and getting to work on reusable, generic validation rules. This is going to be thorough!
---
[2026-02-14T16:52:51.582+0100] Jakub Skurek:
Mission accomplished! Here is what I delivered:

3 new generic validation rules, all reusable across every tag definition:

  * validateDuplicateAttributes - catches repeated attribute names (e.g., lang=csharp lang=js)
  * validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue - catches required attributes with empty values (e.g., title="")
  * validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues - validates positional attributes against allowed values (e.g., banner variant)

Also fixed a bug in page_link suppress_warnings definition (boolean false instead of string) and added number type validation to toc heading level attributes. 17 new test cases written, all 441 tests passing.

Pull Request: https://dev.azure.com/KenticoCustomerSuccess/CustomerEducation/_git/kentico-docs-autocomplete-vscode/pullrequest/2901

See the attached handoff.md for detailed design decisions and the full list of changes. VSIX binary (v1.2.8) is also attached.
---
[2026-02-14T18:11:32.948+0100] Jakub Skurek:
Review comments added to PR
---
[2026-02-14T19:18:37.417+0100] Jakub Skurek:
Revision complete! All items from the previous review feedback have been addressed. Here is what was added:

Two new declarative properties on TagAttribute: mutuallyExclusiveWith (string array for conflicting attributes) and dependsOn (string for required dependency). Two new generic validation rules created: validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes and validateAttributeDependency. Both are registered globally and apply to all tags automatically.

Added dataType to every attribute across all 27 tag definitions, enabling the existing type validation rule to fire properly. Replaced the custom card icon/image conflict validator with declarative mutuallyExclusiveWith. Fixed cell colspan/rowspan dataType, added grid_item allowedParents, added row header/secondaryHeader mutual exclusion, and added code_link id/exclude dependency on source.

452 tests passing (28 new total). Build, lint, and tests all clean. Updated VSIX binary and handoff attached.

PR: https://dev.azure.com/KenticoCustomerSuccess/CustomerEducation/_git/kentico-docs-autocomplete-vscode/pullrequest/2901
---
[2026-03-07T09:19:56.696+0100] Jakub Skurek:
@MalphAutocomplete(branch=ralph/doc-3141) beepboop

```diff
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e9885ad
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that attributes with a `dependsOn` requirement have the dependency attribute present.
+ * For example, code_link's `id` attribute depends on `source` being present.
+ */
+export const validateAttributeDependency: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.dependsOn) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const dependencyLower = attrDef.dependsOn.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(dependencyLower)) {
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' requires '${attrDef.dependsOn}' to be present on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DEP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..37800f9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
+ * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
+ */
+export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;
+
+    // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
+    const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
+    if (!tagNameMatch) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
+    // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
+    const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
+    const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count
+
+    let match;
+    while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
+        const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
+        seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
+    }
+
+    for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
+        if (count > 1) {
+            // Find the original casing from the tag text
+            const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
+            const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
+            const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;
+
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts

@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
+ * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
+ */
+export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.required) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (attrDef.isPositional) {
+            // Check positional argv1 for empty value
+            const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+            if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+                diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                    tagInstance.tagRange,
+                    `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                    vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                    `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                ));
+            }
+        } else {
+            // Check named required attributes for empty values
+            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
+                const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
+                if (value.trim() === '') {
+                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        valueRange,
+                        `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                        `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3c6818d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that mutually exclusive attributes are not used together on the same tag.
+ * Uses the `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on TagAttribute definitions.
+ * Reports each conflict pair only once using alphabetical ordering.
+ */
+export const validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+    const reportedPairs = new Set<string>();
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith || attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith.length === 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        for (const conflictName of attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith) {
+            const conflictNameLower = conflictName.toLowerCase();
+            if (presentAttrsLower.has(conflictNameLower)) {
+                // Use alphabetical ordering to report each conflict only once
+                const pairKey = [attrNameLower, conflictNameLower].sort().join('|');
+                if (!reportedPairs.has(pairKey)) {
+                    reportedPairs.add(pairKey);
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        tagInstance.tagRange,
+                        `Attributes '${attrDef.name}' and '${conflictName}' cannot be used together on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                        `TAG-EXCLUSIVE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..396feca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates the value of positional attributes (argv1) against the allowed values list
+ * defined in the tag's positional attribute definition.
+ * 
+ * This complements validateTagAttributeAllowedValues which only checks named attributes.
+ * Tags like banner have a positional 'variant' with allowed values that need validation.
+ */
+export const validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    // Find positional attribute definition with allowed values
+    const positionalAttrDef = tagDefinition.attributes.find(
+        attr => attr.isPositional && attr.values && Array.isArray(attr.values) && attr.values.length > 0
+    );
+
+    if (!positionalAttrDef) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+
+    // Only validate if a positional value is actually present
+    if (positionalValue === undefined || positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const allowedValuesSet = new Set(positionalAttrDef.values!.map(String));
+
+    if (!allowedValuesSet.has(positionalValue)) {
+        const allowedList = positionalAttrDef.values!.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
+        diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+            tagInstance.tagRange,
+            `Invalid value '${positionalValue}' for positional attribute '${positionalAttrDef.name}' in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
+            vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+            `TAG-POS-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${positionalAttrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+        ));
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
```

```diff
+import * as assert from 'assert';

+async function buildContext(
+    tagText: string,

+    options?: { isClosingTag?: boolean }

+    const document = await vscode.workspace.openTextDocument({ content: tagText, language: 'markdown' });
+    const isClosing = options?.isClosingTag ?? false;
+    const tagRange = new vscode.Range(
+        new vscode.Position(0, 0),
+        new vscode.Position(0, tagText.length)
+    );
+
+    const attributes = isClosing ? {} : TagUtils.parseAttributes(tagText);
+
+    const tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo = {
+        tagText,
+        tagRange,
+        attributes,
+        isOpeningTag: !isClosing,
+        isClosingTag: isClosing,
+        indentation: 0,
+        tagName: tagDefinition.tagName
+    };
+
+    return {
+        document,
+        editor: {} as vscode.TextEditor, // Not used by these rules
+        tagDefinition,
+        tagInstance
+    };
+}

+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 2);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag attributes without dependsOn', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code_link lang=ts %}', codeLinkDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endcode_link %}', codeLinkDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+});
```

```diff
diff --git a/.github/copilot-instructions.md b/.github/copilot-instructions.md

--- a/.github/copilot-instructions.md
+++ b/.github/copilot-instructions.md
@@ -89,7 +89,21 @@ Dynamic completions use `loadSupportedValues` functions - see `src/definitions/h
 ### Validation Rules
 - **Per-instance**: `TagValidationRuleFn` in tag's `validationRules[]`
 - **Document-level**: `TagDocumentValidationRuleFn` in `documentValidationRules[]`
+- **Generic rules** in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/` — applied to all tags automatically:
+  - `validateRequiredTagAttribute` — missing required attributes
+  - `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` — required attributes present but empty
+  - `validateTagAttributeAllowedValues` — named attribute values against allowed list
+  - `validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues` — positional (argv1) values against allowed list
+  - `validateDuplicateAttributes` — same attribute name used multiple times
+  - `validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes` — conflicting attributes used together (via `mutuallyExclusiveWith`)
+  - `validateAttributeDependency` — attribute used without its required dependency (via `dependsOn`)
+  - `validateTagAttributeType` — attribute value type checking
+  - `validateUnknownTagAttribute` — unrecognized attribute names
+  - `validateAllowedParent` — parent tag context
+  - `validatePairTagIndentation` — opening/closing tag indentation match
+  - `validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag` — list before closing tag
 - Rules return `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
+- Register new generic rules in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`
 
 ### Internal Events
 Event-driven updates via `src/logic/events/internalEventEmitter.ts`:
diff --git a/CHANGELOG.md b/CHANGELOG.md
index 0379b42..1748769 100644
--- a/CHANGELOG.md
+++ b/CHANGELOG.md
@@ -4,6 +4,23 @@ All notable changes to the "kc-mdcompletions" extension will be documented in th
 
 Check [Keep a Changelog](http://keepachangelog.com/) for recommendations on how to structure this file.
 
+## [1.2.8]
+
+- Added generic **duplicate attribute detection** validation rule — warns when the same attribute appears multiple times in a tag.
+- Added generic **empty required attribute value** validation rule — errors when a required attribute is present but has an empty value (e.g., `title=""`).
+- Added generic **positional attribute allowed values** validation rule — validates positional attribute values (like banner `variant`) against their defined allowed values list.
+- Added generic **mutually exclusive attributes** validation rule — warns when conflicting attributes are used together (e.g., card `icon` and `image`). Uses declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on `TagAttribute`.
+- Added generic **attribute dependency** validation rule — warns when an attribute is used without its required dependency (e.g., code_link `id` without `source`). Uses declarative `dependsOn` property on `TagAttribute`.
+- Extended `TagAttribute` interface with `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn` declarative properties for metadata-driven validation.
+- Replaced custom `validateCardIconImageConflict` rule with declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith` on card's `icon`/`image` attributes.
+- Added `dataType` to all tag definition attributes across all 27 tag definitions for comprehensive type validation.
+- Added `allowedParents: [GRID]` to `grid_item` tag definition.
+- Fixed `cell` tag `colspan`/`rowspan` `dataType` from `String` to `Number`.[m
+- Added `mutuallyExclusiveWith` between `row` tag's `header` and `secondaryHeader` attributes.
+- Added `dependsOn: 'source'` to `code_link` tag's `id` and `exclude` attributes.
+- Fixed `page_link` tag `suppress_warnings` attribute definition — corrected unquoted `false` boolean to string `'false'` in allowed values.
+- Added `dataType: Number` to `toc` tag's `minHeadingLevel` and `maxHeadingLevel` attributes for type validation.
+
 ## [1.2.7]
 
 - aira_instructions tag support.
```

```
import * as vscode from 'vscode'; // Import vscode for DiagnosticSeverity

import { TagNames } from "../../../../../constants";
import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/helpers';
import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
// --- Define the Tag-Specific Validation Rule ---

export const validateCardIconImageConflict: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    // Check only applies to the 'card' tag's opening instance
    if (tagDefinition.tagName === TagNames.CARD && tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        // Case-insensitive check for attribute existence using parsed attributes
        const hasImage = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'image');
        const hasIcon = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'icon');

        if (hasImage && hasIcon) {
             // Try to highlight the whole tag; refining range might be complex here
             diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                tagInstance.tagRange,
                `Card tag should define either 'icon' or 'image', not both.`,
                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Conflict is a Warning
                `TAG-CARD-CONFLICT` // Unique code for this diagnostic
            ));
        }
    }
    return diagnostics;
};
<exited with exit code 0>
```

```
## Scout Report — DOC-3141

### PR
- PR ID: 2901
- PR URL: https://dev.azure.com/anthropic/kentico-docs/_git/kentico-docs-autocomplete-vscode/pullrequest/2901
- Title: DOC-3141 - Add generic tag attribute validation rules
- Branch: `ralph/doc-3141` → `main`
- Author: Jakub Skurek
- Status: Active (not draft)

### Summary
This PR adds 5 new generic tag attribute validation rules (applied automatically to all tags via `rulesRegister.ts`), extends `TagAttribute` with two new declarative properties (`mutuallyExclusiveWith`, `dependsOn`), adds `dataType` to all attributes across all 27 tag definitions, replaces one custom per-tag validation rule with the generic declarative approach, and includes comprehensive tests (340 lines).

### Pattern Checklist

- [x] **TagAttribute interface extended**: `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn` added to `src/definitions/tags/types.ts` (lines 114-124)
- [x] **Rules registered**: All 5 new rules imported and added to `allTagValidationRules[]` in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`
- [x] **Rules follow `TagValidationRuleFn` signature**: Each accepts `TagValidationContext`, returns `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
- [x] **Rules use `createTagDiagnostic` helper**: All rules use the standard helper from `../helpers`
- [x] **Rules skip closing tags**: All 5 rules have `if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) return diagnostics;` guard
- [x] **Custom rule migrated to declarative**: Card's `validateCardIconImageConflict` replaced by `mutuallyExclusiveWith` on icon/image attributes
- [x] **dataType added to all tag definitions**: All 27 tag definition files updated with appropriate `AttributeDataType` values
- [x] **Version bumped**: 1.2.7 → 1.2.8 in `package.json`
- [x] **CHANGELOG updated**: Full 1.2.8 entry with all changes documented
- [x] **Copilot instructions updated**: Documents all generic rules and registration pattern
- [N/A] **TagNames enum**: No new tags — N/A
- [N/A] **Grammar updated**: No grammar changes — N/A
- [N/A] **Disposal**: No new disposables — N/A

### Build Status
- **Compile**: ✅ PASS — webpack compiled successfully in 5247ms, no errors
- **Lint**: ✅ PASS — ESLint with `--max-warnings 0` passed (TypeScript version warning is cosmetic, not a lint failure)
- **Tests**: ✅ PASS — **452 passing** (861ms), 0 failing

### Notable Observations

1. **Old custom rule file not deleted**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` still exists on disk and exports `validateCardIconImageConflict`. The import was removed from `card.types.ts` so it's dead code. The file is not in the diff (not deleted). This is harmless (lint passes, no unused import warnings since the file itself isn't imported anywhere) but is orphaned code.

2. **pageLink `suppress_warnings` bugfix**: The `values` array had an unquoted `false` boolean literal (`values: ['true', false]`) which was corrected to `values: ['true', 'false']`. This was a latent type mismatch bug — the `values` field type is `(string | number | boolean)[]` so it compiled, but the validation rule comparison would have failed since `false !== 'false'`.

3. **cell colspan/rowspan dataType fix**: Changed from `AttributeDataType.String` to `AttributeDataType.Number`. The `values` arrays (`[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`) are number literals, so `Number` is correct.

4. **gridItem `allowedParents`**: Added `allowedParents: [TagNames.GRID]` — this is a correctness improvement (grid_item should only appear inside grid).

5. **codeLink retains `validationRules`**: The codeLink tag still has per-tag `validationRules` for source/section/exclude existence checking. These are content-aware rules that can't be generalized, so this is correct.

6. **Duplicate attribute detection uses regex on raw tag text**: The `validateDuplicateAttributes` rule re-parses the raw tag text rather than relying on `tagInstance.attributes` (which is a dictionary and would lose duplicates). This is the correct approach.

7. **Import of `TagValidationContext` in some rules**: Both `validateDuplicateAttributes` and `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` import `TagValidationContext` but only use `TagValidationRuleFn` from the types. The `context` parameter is typed via `TagValidationRuleFn`, so the `TagValidationContext` import is unused. However, lint passes with `--max-warnings 0`, so TypeScript/ESLint are apparently not flagging this (possibly the type is used for destructuring inference).

### Missing Connections

1. **Orphaned file**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` is no longer imported anywhere but was not deleted. No functional impact but is dead code.

### Focus Areas for Reviewers

1. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts`** — Most complex of the 5 new rules. Uses regex-based parsing of raw tag text to find duplicates. Verify the regex `\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)` correctly handles all attribute name patterns and edge cases (e.g., attribute values that contain `=`).

2. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts`** — Handles both positional (argv1) and named required attributes. Verify the empty-value check logic for positional attributes and whether `undefined` vs empty string is handled correctly at all call sites.

3. **`src/definitions/tags/types.ts`** — Core interface change adding `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn`. Verify these properties are sufficient for the use cases and whether the types (string[] vs string) are correct for future expansion.

4. **`src/definitions/tags/block/card/card.types.ts`** — Migration from custom `validateCardIconImageConflict` to declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith`. Verify the diagnostic messages/codes remain consistent or are intentionally changed.

5. **`src/definitions/tags/links/pageLink/pageLink.types.ts`** — Contains the `values: ['true', false]` → `['true', 'false']` bugfix. Verify this is the correct fix and no other tag definitions have similar unquoted boolean values.

6. **`src/definitions/tags/tables/cell/cell.types.ts`** — `colspan`/`rowspan` dataType changed from `String` to `Number`. Verify the existing `validateTagAttributeType` rule handles this correctly with the `values: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]` number array.

7. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`** — Verify rule ordering is intentional. The new rules are inserted between existing rules.

8. **`src/test/diagnostics/tagAttributeValidation.test.ts`** — 340 lines of tests for all 5 rules. Verify edge case coverage is sufficient (e.g., case sensitivity, whitespace, multiple diagnostics on the same tag).

9. **Orphaned file `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts`** — Should this be deleted?
```

```diff
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e9885ad

@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that attributes with a `dependsOn` requirement have the dependency attribute present.
+ * For example, code_link's `id` attribute depends on `source` being present.
+ */
+export const validateAttributeDependency: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.dependsOn) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const dependencyLower = attrDef.dependsOn.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(dependencyLower)) {
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' requires '${attrDef.dependsOn}' to be present on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DEP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..37800f9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
+ * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
+ */
+export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;
+
+    // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
+    const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
+    if (!tagNameMatch) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
+    // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
+    const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
+    const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count
+
+    let match;
+    while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
+        const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
+        seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
+    }
+
+    for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
+        if (count > 1) {
+            // Find the original casing from the tag text
+            const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
+            const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
+            const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;
+
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4d5bc70
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
+ * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
+ */
+export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.required) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (attrDef.isPositional) {
+            // Check positional argv1 for empty value
+            const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+            if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+                diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                    tagInstance.tagRange,
+                    `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                    vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                    `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                ));
+            }
+        } else {
+            // Check named required attributes for empty values
+            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
+                const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
+                if (value.trim() === '') {
+                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        valueRange,
+                        `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                        `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3c6818d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that mutually exclusive attributes are not used together on the same tag.
+ * Uses the `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on TagAttribute definitions.
+ * Reports each conflict pair only once using alphabetical ordering.
+ */
+export const validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+    const reportedPairs = new Set<string>();
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith || attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith.length === 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        for (const conflictName of attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith) {
+            const conflictNameLower = conflictName.toLowerCase();
+            if (presentAttrsLower.has(conflictNameLower)) {
+                // Use alphabetical ordering to report each conflict only once
+                const pairKey = [attrNameLower, conflictNameLower].sort().join('|');
+                if (!reportedPairs.has(pairKey)) {
+                    reportedPairs.add(pairKey);
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        tagInstance.tagRange,
+                        `Attributes '${attrDef.name}' and '${conflictName}' cannot be used together on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                        `TAG-EXCLUSIVE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..396feca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates the value of positional attributes (argv1) against the allowed values list
+ * defined in the tag's positional attribute definition.
+ * 
+ * This complements validateTagAttributeAllowedValues which only checks named attributes.
+ * Tags like banner have a positional 'variant' with allowed values that need validation.
+ */
+export const validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    // Find positional attribute definition with allowed values
+    const positionalAttrDef = tagDefinition.attributes.find(
+        attr => attr.isPositional && attr.values && Array.isArray(attr.values) && attr.values.length > 0
+    );
+
+    if (!positionalAttrDef) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+
+    // Only validate if a positional value is actually present
+    if (positionalValue === undefined || positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const allowedValuesSet = new Set(positionalAttrDef.values!.map(String));
+
+    if (!allowedValuesSet.has(positionalValue)) {
+        const allowedList = positionalAttrDef.values!.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
+        diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+            tagInstance.tagRange,
+            `Invalid value '${positionalValue}' for positional attribute '${positionalAttrDef.name}' in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
+            vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+            `TAG-POS-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${positionalAttrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+        ));
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
```

```diff
@@ -0,0 +1,340 @@
+import * as assert from 'assert';
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagNames } from '../../constants';
+import { TagDefinition, TagAttribute } from '../../definitions/tags/types';
+import { TagInstanceInfo, TagValidationContext } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
+import { TagUtils } from '../../logic/_helpers/tagUtils';
+import { validateDuplicateAttributes } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule';
+import { validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule';
+import { validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule';
+import { validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule';
+import { validateAttributeDependency } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule';
+
+// Helper to create a test document and build a validation context
+async function buildContext(
+    tagText: string,
+    tagDefinition: TagDefinition,
+    options?: { isClosingTag?: boolean }
+): Promise<TagValidationContext> {
+    const document = await vscode.workspace.openTextDocument({ content: tagText, language: 'markdown' });
+    const isClosing = options?.isClosingTag ?? false;
+    const tagRange = new vscode.Range(
+        new vscode.Position(0, 0),
+        new vscode.Position(0, tagText.length)
+    );
+
+    const attributes = isClosing ? {} : TagUtils.parseAttributes(tagText);
+
+    const tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo = {
+        tagText,
+        tagRange,
+        attributes,
+        isOpeningTag: !isClosing,
+        isClosingTag: isClosing,
+        indentation: 0,
+        tagName: tagDefinition.tagName
+    };
+
+    return {
+        document,
+        editor: {} as vscode.TextEditor, // Not used by these rules
+        tagDefinition,
+        tagInstance
+    };
+}
+
+// Minimal snippet provider for test definitions
+const dummySnippet = async () => ({} as vscode.CompletionItem);
+
+// ========================================================
+// validateDuplicateAttributes Tests
+// ========================================================
+suite('validateDuplicateAttributes', () => {
+
+    const testDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.CODE,
+        isPairTag: true,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'lang', required: true },
+            { name: 'title', required: false },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect duplicate named attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code lang=csharp lang=js %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateDuplicateAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('lang'));
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('2 times'));
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag unique attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code lang=csharp title="test" %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateDuplicateAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag tag with no attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateDuplicateAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endcode %}', testDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validateDuplicateAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should detect duplicate with different casing', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code Lang=csharp lang=js %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateDuplicateAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+    });
+});
+
+// ========================================================
+// validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue Tests
+// ========================================================
+suite('validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue', () => {
+
+    const testDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.CARD,
+        isPairTag: true,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'title', required: true },
+            { name: 'link', required: true },
+            { name: 'image', required: false },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect empty required named attribute', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="" link="http://x" %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('title'));
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('empty'));
+    });
+
+    test('should detect multiple empty required attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="" link="" %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 2);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag non-empty required attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="My Card" link="http://x" %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag empty optional attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="My Card" link="http://x" image="" %}', testDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endcard %}', testDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should detect empty positional required attribute', async () => {
+        const positionalDef: TagDefinition = {
+            tagName: TagNames.IMAGE,
+            isPairTag: false,
+            snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+            attributes: [
+                { name: 'src', required: true, isPositional: true },
+                { name: 'title', required: true },
+            ]
+        };
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% image "" title="test" %}', positionalDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('src'));
+    });
+});
+
+// ========================================================
+// validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues Tests
+// ========================================================
+suite('validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues', () => {
+
+    const bannerDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.BANNER,
+        isPairTag: true,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'variant', required: false, isPositional: true, values: ['primary', 'secondary'] },
+            { name: 'title', required: true },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect invalid positional attribute value', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% banner tertiary title="Test" %}', bannerDef);
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('tertiary'));
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('variant'));
+    });
+
+    test('should accept valid positional attribute value', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% banner primary title="Test" %}', bannerDef);
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should accept secondary as valid', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% banner secondary title="Test" %}', bannerDef);
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag when no positional value provided', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% banner title="Test" %}', bannerDef);
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endbanner %}', bannerDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip when no positional attr has allowed values', async () => {
+        const noValuesDef: TagDefinition = {
+            tagName: TagNames.IMAGE,
+            isPairTag: false,
+            snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+            attributes: [
+                { name: 'src', required: true, isPositional: true },
+            ]
+        };
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% image anything.png %}', noValuesDef);
+        const diagnostics = validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+});
+
+// ========================================================
+// validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes Tests
+// ========================================================
+suite('validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes', () => {
+
+    const cardDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.CARD,
+        isPairTag: true,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'title', required: true },
+            { name: 'link', required: true },
+            { name: 'image', required: false, mutuallyExclusiveWith: ['icon'] },
+            { name: 'icon', required: false, mutuallyExclusiveWith: ['image'] },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect conflicting attributes', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="x" link="y" image=hero icon=star %}', cardDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('image'));
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('icon'));
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag when only one of the exclusive pair is present', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="x" link="y" image=hero %}', cardDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag when neither exclusive attribute is present', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="x" link="y" %}', cardDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endcard %}', cardDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should report each conflict pair only once', async () => {
+        // Both image and icon have mutuallyExclusiveWith pointing to each other
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% card title="x" link="y" image=hero icon=star %}', cardDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        // Should only produce 1 diagnostic, not 2
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+    });
+
+    const rowDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.ROW,
+        isPairTag: true,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'header', required: false, mutuallyExclusiveWith: ['secondaryHeader'] },
+            { name: 'secondaryHeader', required: false, mutuallyExclusiveWith: ['header'] },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect row header/secondaryHeader conflict', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% row header=true secondaryHeader=true %}', rowDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+    });
+});
+
+// ========================================================
+// validateAttributeDependency Tests
+// ========================================================
+suite('validateAttributeDependency', () => {
+
+    const codeLinkDef: TagDefinition = {
+        tagName: TagNames.CODE_LINK,
+        isPairTag: false,
+        snippetProvider: dummySnippet,
+        attributes: [
+            { name: 'source', required: true },
+            { name: 'id', required: false, dependsOn: 'source' },
+            { name: 'exclude', required: false, dependsOn: 'source' },
+            { name: 'lang', required: false },
+        ]
+    };
+
+    test('should detect attribute used without its dependency', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code_link id=mySection %}', codeLinkDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 1);
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('id'));
+        assert.ok(diagnostics[0].message.includes('source'));
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag when dependency is present', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code_link source=file.ts id=mySection %}', codeLinkDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should detect multiple missing dependencies', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code_link id=mySection exclude=other %}', codeLinkDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 2);
+    });
+
+    test('should not flag attributes without dependsOn', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% code_link lang=ts %}', codeLinkDef);
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+
+    test('should skip closing tags', async () => {
+        const ctx = await buildContext('{% endcode_link %}', codeLinkDef, { isClosingTag: true });
+        const diagnostics = validateAttributeDependency(ctx);
+        assert.strictEqual(diagnostics.length, 0);
+    });
+});
```

````
# Copilot Instructions - Kentico Documentation Markdown Completions

VS Code extension providing autocomplete, diagnostics, and decorations for Kentico-flavored Markdown (KFM).

## Architecture Overview

This extension follows a **definition-driven architecture** where tag/attribute behaviors are declared in definition files, then consumed by centralized providers:

```
src/
├── definitions/         # Declarative definitions (tags, headers, YAML)
│   ├── tags/           # Tag definitions by category (block, inline, links, etc.)
│   ├── header/         # YAML frontmatter attribute definitions
│   └── yaml/           # YAML file completions for config files
├── logic/              # Runtime logic consuming definitions
│   ├── completions/    # Completion providers (snippets, attributes, values)
│   ├── diagnostics/    # Validation rules and diagnostic handlers
│   ├── decorations/    # Editor decorations (highlights, gutters)
│   ├── events/         # Internal event emitter system
│   └── _helpers/       # Tag parsing utilities (TagUtils, tagScanner)
└── constants.ts        # TagNames enum, regex patterns, symbols
```

## Adding a New Tag

1. **Add to `TagNames` enum** in `src/constants.ts`
2. **Create definition folder** under `src/definitions/tags/{category}/{tagName}/`
3. **Create `{tagName}.types.ts`** implementing `TagDefinition` interface:
   ```typescript
   export const myTagDefinition: TagDefinition = {
       tagName: TagNames.MY_TAG,
       isPairTag: true,  // requires {% endmy_tag %}
       attributes: [...],
       snippetProvider: myTagSnippet,
       validationRules: [...],  // optional per-instance rules
       decorationProviders: [...],  // optional decorations
   };
   ```
4. **Register in `definitionInit.ts`** - import and add to `definitionsToRegister` array

## Adding a Header Attribute

Header attributes handle YAML frontmatter completions/diagnostics (title, license, related_pages, etc.):

1. **Create folder** under `src/definitions/header/{attributeName}/`
2. **Create `attributeDefinition.ts`** implementing `HeaderAttribute`:
   ```typescript
   export const myAttributeDefinition: HeaderAttribute = {
       name: 'my_attribute',
       required: false,
       description: 'Description for hover',
       dataType: AttributeDataType.String,
       loadSupportedValues: myDataProvider,  // optional dynamic values
       getMissingAttributeCompletion: myMissingProvider,  // optional custom snippet
       validationRules: [...],  // optional
   };
   ```
3. **Register in `headerDefinition.ts`** - add to `markdownHeaderDefinition.attributes[]`

For dynamic values, create a `*DataProvider.ts` that loads from workspace files (see `licenseDataProvider.ts`).

## Decoration System

Decorations provide visual feedback in the editor (highlights, gutter icons, hints):

1. **Add decoration type name** to `DecorationTypeName` union in `src/definitions/tags/types.ts`
2. **Register decoration style** in `src/logic/decorations/decorationTypeManager.ts` - define the `vscode.TextEditorDecorationType`
3. **Create provider function** implementing `TagDecorationProviderFn`:
   ```typescript
   export function myDecorations(
       tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo,
       document: vscode.TextDocument
   ): vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined {
       // Return decoration options for ranges within this tag
   }
   ```
4. **Add to tag's `decorationProviders[]`** array linking `decorationTypeName` to `provider`

Example: `src/definitions/tags/block/code/codeDecorations.ts` provides line highlighting for `{% code %}` blocks.

## Key Patterns

### Definition Registry
All tag definitions register via `registerTagDefinition()` in `src/definitions/definitionRegister.ts`. Access definitions with `getTagDefinition(TagNames.X)` or `getAllTagDefinitions()`.

### Attribute Value Providers
Dynamic completions use `loadSupportedValues` functions - see `src/definitions/header/license/licenseDataProvider.ts` for loading from workspace files.

### Validation Rules
- **Per-instance**: `TagValidationRuleFn` in tag's `validationRules[]`
- **Document-level**: `TagDocumentValidationRuleFn` in `documentValidationRules[]`
- **Generic rules** in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/` — applied to all tags automatically:
  - `validateRequiredTagAttribute` — missing required attributes
  - `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` — required attributes present but empty
  - `validateTagAttributeAllowedValues` — named attribute values against allowed list
  - `validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues` — positional (argv1) values against allowed list
  - `validateDuplicateAttributes` — same attribute name used multiple times
  - `validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes` — conflicting attributes used together (via `mutuallyExclusiveWith`)
  - `validateAttributeDependency` — attribute used without its required dependency (via `dependsOn`)
  - `validateTagAttributeType` — attribute value type checking
  - `validateUnknownTagAttribute` — unrecognized attribute names
  - `validateAllowedParent` — parent tag context
  - `validatePairTagIndentation` — opening/closing tag indentation match
  - `validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag` — list before closing tag
- Rules return `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
- Register new generic rules in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`

### Internal Events
Event-driven updates via `src/logic/events/internalEventEmitter.ts`:
- `activationComplete`, `editorChanged`, `documentChanged`, `selectionChanged`, `configChanged`
- Subscribe in `src/logic/eventSubscriber.ts`

### Tag Parsing
Use `TagUtils` from `src/logic/_helpers/tagUtils.ts`:
- `findEnclosingTagRange()` - get tag context at cursor
- `parseAttributes()` - extract named/positional attrs
- `isInsideOpeningTagDefinition()` - check cursor location

## code_link Tag Implementation

The `{% code_link %}` tag is a complex feature with multiple providers. Reference implementation in `src/definitions/tags/block/codeLink/`:

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `codeLink.types.ts` | Tag definition with attributes (source, id, exclude, lang, etc.) |
| `codeLinkService.ts` | Shared utilities (path resolution, section extraction, validation) |
| `codeLinkValidation.ts` | Validation rules for source file and section markers |
| `codeLinkIdDataProvider.ts` | Autocomplete for `id`/`exclude` attrs (scans source for `//Include:` markers) |
| `codeLinkSourceDataProvider.ts` | Autocomplete for `source` attr (scans code_link_include_root) |
| `codeLinkCodeLens.ts` | CodeLens in markdown showing file preview on hover |
| `codeLinkSourceCodeLens.ts` | CodeLens in source files showing reference counts |
| `codeLinkReferenceScanner.ts` | Scans documentation folders for code_link usage |
| `codeLinkDefinitionProvider.ts` | F12 "Go to Definition" for source attribute |
| `codeLinkDecorations.ts` | Editor decorations for code_link tags |

### CodeLens Registration
CodeLens providers are registered via `src/logic/codelens/codeLensRegister.ts` which is called from `pluginInit.ts`.

## Testing

Tests are in `src/test/` using Mocha. Run with `npm run test:xvfb` (Linux) or `npm test`.

- Tests use pure utility functions from `codeLinkTestUtils.ts` (no VS Code API dependency)
- Test files organized by feature: `validation.test.ts`, `highlight.test.ts`, `referenceScanner.test.ts`, etc.
- First-time Linux setup: `npm run test:setup` installs xvfb dependencies

## Development Commands

```bash
npm run watch      # Compile with webpack in watch mode
npm run lint       # ESLint
npm run test       # Run tests (requires compile-tests first)
npm run test:xvfb  # Run tests in headless mode (Linux)
npm run build      # Package VSIX
```

**Debug**: Press F5 to launch Extension Development Host. Extension activates when workspace contains `**/src/_configs/_config_primary.yml`.

## Key Files for Context

- Tag syntax patterns: `src/constants.ts` (`CODE_START_REGEX`, `SYMBOLS`)
- Workspace file loading: `src/logic/filesystem/workspaceFileLoader.ts`
- Grammar injection: `grammars/injections/kfmarkdown.json`
<exited with exit code 0>
````

```
imageAttrConflict.ts
import * as vscode from 'vscode'; // Import vscode for DiagnosticSeverity

import { TagNames } from "../../../../../constants";
import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/helpers';
import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
// --- Define the Tag-Specific Validation Rule ---

export const validateCardIconImageConflict: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    // Check only applies to the 'card' tag's opening instance
    if (tagDefinition.tagName === TagNames.CARD && tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        // Case-insensitive check for attribute existence using parsed attributes
        const hasImage = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'image');
        const hasIcon = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'icon');

        if (hasImage && hasIcon) {
             // Try to highlight the whole tag; refining range might be complex here
             diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                tagInstance.tagRange,
                `Card tag should define either 'icon' or 'image', not both.`,
                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Conflict is a Warning
                `TAG-CARD-CONFLICT` // Unique code for this diagnostic
            ));
        }
    }
    return diagnostics;
};
<exited with exit code 0>
```

```
import * as vscode from 'vscode';

import { BaseAttribute } from '../common/types';
import { TagNames } from '../../constants';
import { TagDocumentValidationRuleFn, TagInstanceInfo, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
import { AttributeDefinitionProviderFn } from '../../logic/definitions/types';

// --- Type Signatures specific to Tag attributes ---
export type AttributeValueProviderFn = (
  document: vscode.TextDocument,
  position: vscode.Position,
  tagContext: TagContext, // Uses TagContext
  attrDef: TagAttribute, // Use TagAttribute here
  currentAttributes: Record<string, string> // Tag attributes are usually strings
) => Promise<vscode.CompletionItem[] | undefined>;

/**
 * Defines the signature for functions that generate a `vscode.CompletionItem`
 * used for inserting a tag as a snippet.
 *
 * @async
 * @returns {Promise<vscode.CompletionItem>} A Promise resolving to the completion item for the snippet.
 */
export type SnippetProviderFn = () => Promise<vscode.CompletionItem>;

/**
 * Defines the signature for functions that generate decoration options for a specific tag instance.
 * These functions are typically assigned to the `decorationProvider` property
 * of a {@link TagDefinition} definition.
 *
 * @param {Readonly<TagInstanceInfo>} tagInstance - Information about the specific tag instance found in the document.
 * @param {vscode.TextDocument} document - The text document being processed.
 * @returns {vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined} An array of decoration options to apply to this tag instance, or undefined if no decorations are needed.
 */
export type TagDecorationProviderFn = (
  tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo,
  document: vscode.TextDocument
) => vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined; // Returns an array of decorations for this instance

/**
 * Defines the allowed names for decoration types used in TagDecorationProviderConfig.
 * This ensures type safety when referencing decoration styles.
 * Add new valid decoration type names here.
 */
export type DecorationTypeName =
    | 'codeHighlight' // For code block line highlighting
    | 'codeLinkPreview' // For code_link file preview hover
    | 'gutterIcon'
    | 'relativeLineHint' // For the "(Ln X)" hint shown at the end of the current line in code blocks
    | 'warningBlockBorder' // For styling warning admonition blocks <-- NEW
    | 'airaInstructionsBlockBorder' // For styling AI assistant instruction blocks
    ;

/**
 * Configuration object linking a specific decoration provider function
 * to a named decoration type. Used within the {@link TagDefinition}'s `decorationProviders` array.
 */
export interface TagDecorationProviderConfig {
  /**
   * A unique key identifying the type or style of decoration this provider generates
   * (e.g., 'highlight', 'gutterIcon', 'warningBorder'). This key will be used by the
   * central handler to retrieve the corresponding vscode.TextEditorDecorationType
   * from the DecorationManager.
   */
  decorationTypeName: DecorationTypeName;
  /**
   * The function implementing the TagDecorationProviderFn signature, responsible for
   * generating the actual DecorationOptions for this specific type and tag instance.
   */
  provider: TagDecorationProviderFn;
}

/**
 * Defines the signature for functions that can dynamically provide
 * completion items for the *value* of a specific tag attribute.
 * These functions are typically assigned to the `loadSupportedValues` property
 * of a {@link TagAttribute} definition.
 *
 * @param document The text document being edited.
 * @param position The current cursor position where completion is requested.
 * @param tagContext Contextual information about the enclosing tag instance, provided by {@link TagUtils.findEnclosingTagRange}.
 * @param attrDef The definition of the specific attribute whose value is being completed, from the {@link TagDefinition}.
 * @param currentAttributes A record containing the names and values of attributes already present in the current tag instance, parsed by {@link TagUtils.parseAttributes}.
 * @returns A Promise resolving to an array of {@link vscode.CompletionItem} suggestions for the attribute's value, or `undefined`/empty array if no suggestions apply.
 */
export type AttributeValueProvider = (
  document: vscode.TextDocument,
  position: vscode.Position,
  tagContext: TagContext,
  attrDef: TagAttribute,
  currentAttributes: Record<string, string>
) => Promise<vscode.CompletionItem[] | undefined>;

/**
 * Defines a specific attribute for a Kentico tag.
 * Extends BaseAttribute with tag-specific properties.
 */
export interface TagAttribute extends BaseAttribute { 
  /** Is this attribute specified by position rather than name? (e.g., {% image src %}) */
  isPositional?: boolean;

  /** Should the snippet completion omit '=value'? (e.g., for boolean flags like 'border') */
  omitValueSnippet?: boolean;

  /** Delegate function to load supported values dynamically */
  loadSupportedValues?: AttributeValueProviderFn; // Uses the specific Tag signature

  /** 
   * Optional delegate function for "Go to Definition" (F12) navigation.
   * When the cursor is on this attribute's value and F12 is pressed,
   * this function will be called to resolve the definition location.
   */
  definitionProvider?: AttributeDefinitionProviderFn;

  /**
   * Optional list of attribute names that conflict with this attribute.
   * If any of the listed attributes are also present on the same tag instance,
   * a warning diagnostic will be raised.
   */
  mutuallyExclusiveWith?: string[];

  /**
   * Optional name of another attribute that must be present for this attribute to be valid.
   * If this attribute is used but the dependency attribute is missing, a warning is raised.
   */
  dependsOn?: string;
}

/**
 * Defines the structure, attributes, and completion behavior for a specific Kentico tag.
 */
export interface TagDefinition {
  /**
   * The unique identifier for the tag, using the {@link TagNames} enum.
   * Matches the keyword used after '{%'.
   */
  tagName: TagNames;
  /**
   * An array defining the possible attributes for this tag, using the {@link TagAttribute} interface.
   */
  attributes: TagAttribute[];
  /**
   * Function that generates the `{@link vscode.CompletionItem}`
   * used for inserting this tag as a snippet. See {@link SnippetProviderFn}.
   * If omitted, no basic snippet completion will be offered for this tag
   * by the central snippet provider.
   */
  snippetProvider: SnippetProviderFn;
  /** Optional: Specifies the *only* tag this tag can be a 
   * direct child of for snippet suggestions. */
  allowedParents?: TagNames[];
  /** Set to true if the tag requires a closing {% end... %} 
   * tag (e.g., {% code %}{% endcode %}). Defaults to false if omitted. */
  isPairTag: boolean;
  /**
     * Optional array of validation functions specific to this tag definition.
     * These rules run *in addition* to any globally registered tag validation rules.
     */
  validationRules?: TagValidationRuleFn[];
  /**
    * Optional array of document-level validation functions specific to this tag definition.
    * These rules run once per document during the document-level validation phase,
    * *in addition* to any globally registered document-level tag validation rules.
    * They receive the full list of tags found in the document.
    */
  documentValidationRules?: TagDocumentValidationRuleFn[];

  /**
     * Optional array configuring decoration providers for this tag. Each entry links
     * a provider function to a specific named decoration type (style).
     * See {@link TagDecorationProviderConfig}.
     */
  decorationProviders?: TagDecorationProviderConfig[];
}

/**
 * Represents the context within an opening tag, primarily used for attribute value completions.
 */
export interface TagContext {
  tagContent: string;         // The full text of the tag, e.g., '{% card ... %}'
  tagRange: vscode.Range;     // The range of the tag in the document
  startTagPos: vscode.Position; // The start position of the tag
}
```

```
import { checkAllTablesForMultipleHeaders } from "./docrules/checkAllTablesForMultipleHeaders.rule";
import { checkTagPairing } from "./docrules/checkTagPairing.rule";
import { validateAllowedParent } from "./rules/validateAllowedParent.rule";
import { validateAttributeDependency } from "./rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule";
import { validateDuplicateAttributes } from "./rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule";
import { validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue } from "./rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule";
import { validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes } from "./rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule";
import { validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag } from "./rules/validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag.rule";
import { validatePairTagIndentation } from "./rules/validatePairTagIndentation.rule";
import { validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues } from "./rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule";
import { validateRequiredTagAttribute } from "./rules/validateRequiredTagAttribute.rule";
import { validateTagAttributeAllowedValues } from "./rules/validateTagAttributeAllowedValues.rule";
import { validateTagAttributeType } from "./rules/validateTagAttributeType.rule";
import { validateUnknownTagAttribute } from "./rules/validateUnknownTagAttribute.rule";
import { TagDocumentValidationRuleFn, TagValidationRuleFn } from "./types";

/**
* List of all rule functions to be executed by the tagDiagnosticsHandler.
* The handler will iterate through tags in the document and call each of these rules
* with the appropriate context for that tag instance.
*
* Note: Rules like mismatched/unclosed tags might need special handling or
* to be invoked differently (e.g., once per document) depending on implementation.
*/
export const allTagValidationRules: TagValidationRuleFn[] = [
  // Rules applied per tag instance:
  validateAllowedParent,          // Check if tag is allowed in its parent
  validateRequiredTagAttribute,   // Check for missing required attributes
  validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue, // Check required attributes are not empty
  validateTagAttributeType,       // Check attribute value types
  validateTagAttributeAllowedValues, // Check attribute values against allowed lists
  validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues, // Check positional attribute values against allowed lists
  validateDuplicateAttributes,    // Check for duplicate attribute names
  validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes, // Check conflicting attributes
  validateAttributeDependency,    // Check attribute dependencies
  validatePairTagIndentation,
  validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag,
  validateUnknownTagAttribute,
];

/**
 * List of all *globally applicable document-level* rule functions.
 * These run once after all tags have been processed.
 */
export const allTagDocumentValidationRules: TagDocumentValidationRuleFn[] = [
  checkAllTablesForMultipleHeaders,
  checkTagPairing,
];
```

```
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { TagAttribute, TagDefinition } from '../../../definitions/tags/types'; 
import { TagNames } from '../../../constants';

/**
 * Context passed to each tag validation rule function.
 * Encapsulates all necessary information about the tag instance being checked.
 */
export interface TagValidationContext {
    /** The document being validated. */
    document: vscode.TextDocument;
    // The current editor
    editor: vscode.TextEditor;
    /** The definition object for the tag type (e.g., card, code). */
    tagDefinition: TagDefinition;
    /** Information about the specific tag instance found in the document. */
    tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo
    /** Optional: The TagNames enum value of the immediate enclosing parent tag, if applicable and found. */
    parentTagName?: TagNames; // Using the TagNames enum type directly might require importing it if not already via TagDefinition
    // Consider adding:
    // - Full parsed document structure (if needed by complex rules)?
    // - Stack information (if rules need to know about siblings/nesting depth beyond immediate parent)?
    /**
     * Optional: If the current tagInstance is a closing tag, this property
     * holds information about its corresponding opening tag instance found earlier.
     * Used for rules that need to compare opening and closing tags (e.g., indentation).
     */
    matchingOpeningTag?: TagInstanceInfo;
}

/**
 * Holds key information about a specific tag instance found in the document.
 */
export interface TagInstanceInfo {
    /** The full text of the tag, e.g., '{% card title="x" %}' or '{% endcard %}' */
    tagText: string;
    /** The range (start and end position) of the tag instance in the document. */
    tagRange: vscode.Range;
    /** The parsed attributes (key-value pairs) of the opening tag. Empty for closing tags. */
    attributes: Record<string, string>;
```
