```
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types'; // Adjust import path
import { createTagDiagnostic, checkTagAttributeType, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers'; // Adjust import path

/**
 * Validates the data type of existing attributes based on the TagAttribute definition.
 */
export const validateTagAttributeType: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    // Only check attributes on opening tags
    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        return diagnostics;
    }

    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
        // Check type only if a dataType is defined for the attribute
        if (attrDef.dataType) {
            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
            // Check if the attribute exists in the parsed instance (case-insensitive keys from parser)
            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
                const actualValueString = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
                // Use the helper function to check the type based on the string value
                if (!checkTagAttributeType(actualValueString, attrDef.dataType)) {
                    // Try to find the specific range of the attribute value for better highlighting
                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange; // Fallback to whole tag range

                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                        valueRange,
                        `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' has incorrect type. Expected type '${attrDef.dataType}'.`,
                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Type mismatches are often Warnings
                        `TAG-TYPE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    });

    return diagnostics;
};
<exited with exit code 0>
```

 Check [Keep a Changelog](http://keepachangelog.com/) for recommendations on how to structure this file.
 
+## [1.2.8]
+
+- Added generic **duplicate attribute detection** validation rule — warns when the same attribute appears multiple times in a tag.
+- Added generic **empty required attribute value** validation rule — errors when a required attribute is present but has an empty value (e.g., `title=""`).
+- Added generic **positional attribute allowed values** validation rule — validates positional attribute values (like banner `variant`) against their defined allowed values list.
+- Added generic **mutually exclusive attributes** validation rule — warns when conflicting attributes are used together (e.g., card `icon` and `image`). Uses declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on `TagAttribute`.
+- Added generic **attribute dependency** validation rule — warns when an attribute is used without its required dependency (e.g., code_link `id` without `source`). Uses declarative `dependsOn` property on `TagAttribute`.
+- Extended `TagAttribute` interface with `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn` declarative properties for metadata-driven validation.
+- Replaced custom `validateCardIconImageConflict` rule with declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith` on card's `icon`/`image` attributes.
+- Added `dataType` to all tag definition attributes across all 27 tag definitions for comprehensive type validation.
+- Added `allowedParents: [GRID]` to `grid_item` tag definition.
+- Fixed `cell` tag `colspan`/`rowspan` `dataType` from `String` to `Number`.
+- Added `mutuallyExclusiveWith` between `row` tag's `header` and `secondaryHeader` attributes.
+- Added `dependsOn: 'source'` to `code_link` tag's `id` and `exclude` attributes.
+- Fixed `page_link` tag `suppress_warnings` attribute definition — corrected unquoted `false` boolean to string `'false'` in allowed values.
+- Added `dataType: Number` to `toc` tag's `minHeadingLevel` and `maxHeadingLevel` attributes for type validation.
+
 ## [1.2.7]
 
 - aira_instructions tag support.

export function checkTagAttributeType(actualValueString: string | undefined, expectedType: TagAttribute['dataType']): boolean {
    // If the attribute doesn't exist (value is undefined), type checking doesn't apply here.
    // Required attribute checks handle missing attributes.
    if (actualValueString === undefined) {
        return true;
    }

    const actualTrimmedValue = actualValueString.trim();

    switch (expectedType) {
        case 'string':
            // Any non-empty string is generally considered a valid string attribute value.
            // Specific format validation (like URL, email) would be a separate rule if needed.
            return typeof actualValueString === 'string'; // Check if it's fundamentally a string
        case 'number':
            // Check if the string represents a finite number.
            return !isNaN(parseFloat(actualTrimmedValue)) && isFinite(Number(actualTrimmedValue));
        case 'boolean':
            // Accept 'true' or 'false' (case-insensitive) or empty string for boolean flags
            const lowerValue = actualTrimmedValue.toLowerCase();
            return lowerValue === 'true' || lowerValue === 'false' || actualTrimmedValue === '';

```

58. ### Pattern Checklist
59. 
60. - [x] **TagAttribute interface extended**: `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn` added to `src/definitions/tags/types.ts` (lines 114-124)
61. - [x] **Rules registered**: All 5 new rules imported and added to `allTagValidationRules[]` in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`
62. - [x] **Rules follow `TagValidationRuleFn` signature**: Each accepts `TagValidationContext`, returns `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
63. - [x] **Rules use `createTagDiagnostic` helper**: All rules use the standard helper from `../helpers`
64. - [x] **Rules skip closing tags**: All 5 rules have `if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) return diagnostics;` guard
65. - [x] **Custom rule migrated to declarative**: Card's `validateCardIconImageConflict` replaced by `mutuallyExclusiveWith` on icon/image attributes
66. - [x] **dataType added to all tag definitions**: All 27 tag definition files updated with appropriate `AttributeDataType` values
67. - [x] **Version bumped**: 1.2.7 → 1.2.8 in `package.json`
68. - [x] **CHANGELOG updated**: Full 1.2.8 entry with all changes documented
69. - [x] **Copilot instructions updated**: Documents all generic rules and registration pattern

73. 
74. ### Build Status
75. - **Compile**: ✅ PASS — webpack compiled successfully in 5247ms, no errors
76. - **Lint**: ✅ PASS — ESLint with `--max-warnings 0` passed (TypeScript version warning is cosmetic, not a lint failure)
77. - **Tests**: ✅ PASS — **452 passing** (861ms), 0 failing
78. 
79. ### Notable Observations
80. 
81. 1. **Old custom rule file not deleted**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` still exists on disk and exports `validateCardIconImageConflict`. The import was removed from `card.types.ts` so it's dead code. The file is not in the diff (not deleted). This is harmless (lint passes, no unused import warnings since the file itself isn't imported anywhere) but is orphaned code.
82. 
83. 2. **pageLink `suppress_warnings` bugfix**: The `values` array had an unquoted `false` boolean literal (`values: ['true', false]`) which was corrected to `values: ['true', 'false']`. This was a latent type mismatch bug — the `values` field type is `(string | number | boolean)[]` so it compiled, but the validation rule comparison would have failed since `false !== 'false'`.
84. 
85. 3. **cell colspan/rowspan dataType fix**: Changed from `AttributeDataType.String` to `AttributeDataType.Number`. The `values` arrays (`[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`) are number literals, so `Number` is correct.
86. 
87. 4. **gridItem `allowedParents`**: Added `allowedParents: [TagNames.GRID]` — this is a correctness improvement (grid_item should only appear inside grid).
88. 
89. 5. **codeLink retains `validationRules`**: The codeLink tag still has per-tag `validationRules` for source/section/exclude existence checking. These are content-aware rules that can't be generalized, so this is correct.
90. 
91. 6. **Duplicate attribute detection uses regex on raw tag text**: The `validateDuplicateAttributes` rule re-parses the raw tag text rather than relying on `tagInstance.attributes` (which is a dictionary and would lose duplicates). This is the correct approach.
92. 
93. 7. **Import of `TagValidationContext` in some rules**: Both `validateDuplicateAttributes` and `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` import `TagValidationContext` but only use `TagValidationRuleFn` from the types. The `context` parameter is typed via `TagValidationRuleFn`, so the `TagValidationContext` import is unused. However, lint passes with `--max-warnings 0`, so TypeScript/ESLint are apparently not flagging this (possibly the type is used for destructuring inference).
94. 
95. ### Missing Connections
96. 
97. 1. **Orphaned file**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` is no longer imported anywhere but was not deleted. No functional impact but is dead code.
98. 
99. ### Focus Areas for Reviewers
100. 
101. 1. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts`** — Most complex of the 5 new rules. Uses regex-based parsing of raw tag text to find duplicates. Verify the regex `\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)` correctly handles all attribute name patterns and edge cases (e.g., attribute values that contain `=`).
102. 
103. 2. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts`** — Handles both positional (argv1) and named required attributes. Verify the empty-value check logic for positional attributes and whether `undefined` vs empty string is handled correctly at all call sites.
104. 
105. 3. **`src/definitions/tags/types.ts`** — Core interface change adding `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn`. Verify these properties are sufficient for the use cases and whether the types (string[] vs string) are correct for future expansion.
106. 
107. 4. **`src/definitions/tags/block/card/card.types.ts`** — Migration from custom `validateCardIconImageConflict` to declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith`. Verify the diagnostic messages/codes remain consistent or are intentionally changed.
108. 
109. 5. **`src/definitions/tags/links/pageLink/pageLink.types.ts`** — Contains the `values: ['true', false]` → `['true', 'false']` bugfix. Verify this is the correct fix and no other tag definitions have similar unquoted boolean values.
110. 
111. 6. **`src/definitions/tags/tables/cell/cell.types.ts`** — `colspan`/`rowspan` dataType changed from `String` to `Number`. Verify the existing `validateTagAttributeType` rule handles this correctly with the `values: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]` number array.
112. 
113. 7. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`** — Verify rule ordering is intentional. The new rules are inserted between existing rules.
114. 
115. 8. **`src/test/diagnostics/tagAttributeValidation.test.ts`** — 340 lines of tests for all 5 rules. Verify edge case coverage is sufficient (e.g., case sensitivity, whitespace, multiple diagnostics on the same tag).
116. 
117. 9. **Orphaned file `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts`** — Should this be deleted?
118.

1. # Copilot Instructions - Kentico Documentation Markdown Completions
2. 
3. VS Code extension providing autocomplete, diagnostics, and decorations for Kentico-flavored Markdown (KFM).
4. 
5. ## Architecture Overview
6. 
7. This extension follows a **definition-driven architecture** where tag/attribute behaviors are declared in definition files, then consumed by centralized providers:
8. 
9. ```
10. src/
11. ├── definitions/         # Declarative definitions (tags, headers, YAML)
12. │   ├── tags/           # Tag definitions by category (block, inline, links, etc.)
13. │   ├── header/         # YAML frontmatter attribute definitions
14. │   └── yaml/           # YAML file completions for config files
15. ├── logic/              # Runtime logic consuming definitions
16. │   ├── completions/    # Completion providers (snippets, attributes, values)
17. │   ├── diagnostics/    # Validation rules and diagnostic handlers
18. │   ├── decorations/    # Editor decorations (highlights, gutters)
19. │   ├── events/         # Internal event emitter system
20. │   └── _helpers/       # Tag parsing utilities (TagUtils, tagScanner)
21. └── constants.ts        # TagNames enum, regex patterns, symbols
22. ```
23. 
24. ## Adding a New Tag
25. 
26. 1. **Add to `TagNames` enum** in `src/constants.ts`
27. 2. **Create definition folder** under `src/definitions/tags/{category}/{tagName}/`
28. 3. **Create `{tagName}.types.ts`** implementing `TagDefinition` interface:
29.    ```typescript
30.    export const myTagDefinition: TagDefinition = {
31.        tagName: TagNames.MY_TAG,
32.        isPairTag: true,  // requires {% endmy_tag %}
33.        attributes: [...],
34.        snippetProvider: myTagSnippet,
35.        validationRules: [...],  // optional per-instance rules
36.        decorationProviders: [...],  // optional decorations
37.    };
38.    ```
39. 4. **Register in `definitionInit.ts`** - import and add to `definitionsToRegister` array
40. 
41. ## Adding a Header Attribute
42. 
43. Header attributes handle YAML frontmatter completions/diagnostics (title, license, related_pages, etc.):
44. 
45. 1. **Create folder** under `src/definitions/header/{attributeName}/`
46. 2. **Create `attributeDefinition.ts`** implementing `HeaderAttribute`:
47.    ```typescript
48.    export const myAttributeDefinition: HeaderAttribute = {
49.        name: 'my_attribute',
50.        required: false,
````

</details>

872. +import * as vscode from 'vscode';
873. +import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
874. +import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
875. +
876. +/**
877. + * Validates that attributes with a `dependsOn` requirement have the dependency attribute present.
878. + * For example, code_link's `id` attribute depends on `source` being present.
879. + */
880. +export const validateAttributeDependency: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
881. +    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
882. +    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
883. +
884. +    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
885. +        return diagnostics;
886. +    }
887. +
888. +    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
889. +
890. +    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
891. +        if (!attrDef.dependsOn) {
892. +            return;
893. +        }
894. +
895. +        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
896. +        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
897. +            return;
898. +        }
899. +
900. +        const dependencyLower = attrDef.dependsOn.toLowerCase();
901. +        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(dependencyLower)) {
902. +            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
903. +                tagInstance.tagRange,
904. +                `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' requires '${attrDef.dependsOn}' to be present on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
905. +                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
906. +                `TAG-DEP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
907. +            ));
908. +        }
909. +    });
910. +
911. +    return diagnostics;
912. +};
913. diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
914. new file mode 100644
915. index 0000000..37800f9
916. --- /dev/null
917. +++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
918. @@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
919. +import * as vscode from 'vscode';
920. +import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
921. +import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';
922. +
923. +/**
924. + * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
925. + * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
926. + */
927. +export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
928. +    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
929. +    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
930. +
931. +    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
932. +        return diagnostics;
933. +    }
934. +
935. +    const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;
936. +
937. +    // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
938. +    const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
939. +    if (!tagNameMatch) {
940. +        return diagnostics;
941. +    }
942. +
943. +    const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
944. +    // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
945. +    const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
946. +    const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count
947. +
948. +    let match;
949. +    while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
950. +        const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
951. +        seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
952. +    }
953. +
954. +    for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
955. +        if (count > 1) {
956. +            // Find the original casing from the tag text
957. +            const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
958. +            const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
959. +            const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;
960. +
961. +            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
962. +                tagInstance.tagRange,
963. +                `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
964. +                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
965. +                `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
966. +            ));
967. +        }
968. +    }
969. +
970. +    return diagnostics;
971. +};
972. diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
973. new file mode 100644
974. index 0000000..4d5bc70
975. --- /dev/null
976. +++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
977. @@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
978. +import * as vscode from 'vscode';
979. +import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
980. +import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';
981. +
982. +/**
983. + * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
984. + * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
985. + */
986. +export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
987. +    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
988. +    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
989. +
990. +    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
991. +        return diagnostics;
992. +    }
993. +
994. +    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
995. +        if (!attrDef.required) {
996. +            return;
997. +        }
998. +
999. +        if (attrDef.isPositional) {
1000. +            // Check positional argv1 for empty value
1001. +            const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
1002. +            if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
1003. +                diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
1004. +                    tagInstance.tagRange,
1005. +                    `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
1006. +                    vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
1007. +                    `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
1008. +                ));
1009. +            }
1010. +        } else {
1011. +            // Check named required attributes for empty values
1012. +            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
1013. +            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
1014. +                const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
1015. +                if (value.trim() === '') {
1016. +                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
1017. +                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
1018. +                        valueRange,
1019. +                        `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
1020. +                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
1021. +                        `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
1022. +                    ));
1023. +                }
1024. +            }
1025. +        }
1026. +    });
1027. +
1028. +    return diagnostics;
1029. +};
1030. diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
1031. new file mode 100644
1032. index 0000000..3c6818d
1033. --- /dev/null
1034. +++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
1035. @@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
1036. +import * as vscode from 'vscode';
1037. +import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
1038. +import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
1039. +
1040. +/**
1041. + * Validates that mutually exclusive attributes are not used together on the same tag.
1042. + * Uses the `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on TagAttribute definitions.
1043. + * Reports each conflict pair only once using alphabetical ordering.
1044. + */
1045. +export const validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
1046. +    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
1047. +    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
1048. +
1049. +    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
1050. +        return diagnostics;
1051. +    }
1052. +
1053. +    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
1054. +    const reportedPairs = new Set<string>();
1055. +
1056. +    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
1057. +        if (!attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith || attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith.length === 0) {
1058. +            return;
1059. +        }
1060. +
1061. +        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
1062. +        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
1063. +            return;
1064. +        }
1065. +
1066. +        for (const conflictName of attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith) {
1067. +            const conflictNameLower = conflictName.toLowerCase();
1068. +            if (presentAttrsLower.has(conflictNameLower)) {
1069. +                // Use alphabetical ordering to report each conflict only once
1070. +                const pairKey = [attrNameLower, conflictNameLower].sort().join('|');
1071. +                if (!reportedPairs.has(pairKey)) {
1072. +                    reportedPairs.add(pairKey);
1073. +                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
1074. +                        tagInstance.tagRange,
1075. +                        `Attributes '${attrDef.name}' and '${conflictName}' cannot be used together on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
1076. +                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
1077. +                        `TAG-EXCLUSIVE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}`
1078. +                    ));
1079. +                }
1080. +            }
1081. +        }
1082. +    });
1083. +
1084. +    return diagnostics;
1085. +};

1100. + * This complements validateTagAttributeAllowedValues which only checks named attributes.
1101. + * Tags like banner have a positional 'variant' with allowed values that need validation.
1102. + */
1103. +export const validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
1104. +    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
1105. +    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
1106. +
1107. +    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
1108. +        return diagnostics;
1109. +    }
1110. +
1111. +    // Find positional attribute definition with allowed values
1112. +    const positionalAttrDef = tagDefinition.attributes.find(
1113. +        attr => attr.isPositional && attr.values && Array.isArray(attr.values) && attr.values.length > 0
1114. +    );
1115. +
1116. +    if (!positionalAttrDef) {
1117. +        return diagnostics;
1118. +    }
1119. +
1120. +    const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
1121. +
1122. +    // Only validate if a positional value is actually present
1123. +    if (positionalValue === undefined || positionalValue.trim() === '') {
1124. +        return diagnostics;
1125. +    }
1126. +
1127. +    const allowedValuesSet = new Set(positionalAttrDef.values!.map(String));
1128. +
1129. +    if (!allowedValuesSet.has(positionalValue)) {
1130. +        const allowedList = positionalAttrDef.values!.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
1131. +        diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
1132. +            tagInstance.tagRange,
1133. +            `Invalid value '${positionalValue}' for positional attribute '${positionalAttrDef.name}' in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
1134. +            vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
1135. +            `TAG-POS-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${positionalAttrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
1136. +        ));
1137. +    }
1138. +
1139. +    return diagnostics;
1140. +};

1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
3. import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
7.  * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
8.  */
9. export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
10.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
11.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
12. 
13.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         return diagnostics;
15.     }
16. 
17.     const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;
18. 
19.     // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
20.     const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
21.     if (!tagNameMatch) {
22.         return diagnostics;
23.     }
24. 
25.     const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
26.     // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
27.     const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
28.     const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count
29. 
30.     let match;
31.     while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
32.         const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
33.         seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
34.     }
35. 
36.     for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
37.         if (count > 1) {
38.             // Find the original casing from the tag text
39.             const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
40.             const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
41.             const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;
42. 
43.             diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
44.                 tagInstance.tagRange,
45.                 `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
46.                 vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
47.                 `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
48.             ));
49.         }
50.     }
51. 
52.     return diagnostics;
53. };
54.
```

</details>

```
1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
3. import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
7.  * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
8.  */
9. export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
10.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
11.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
12. 
13.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         return diagnostics;
15.     }
16. 
17.     tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
18.         if (!attrDef.required) {
19.             return;
20.         }
21. 
22.         if (attrDef.isPositional) {
23.             // Check positional argv1 for empty value
24.             const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
25.             if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
26.                 diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
27.                     tagInstance.tagRange,
28.                     `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
29.                     vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
30.                     `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
31.                 ));
32.             }
33.         } else {
34.             // Check named required attributes for empty values
35.             const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
36.             if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
37.                 const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
38.                 if (value.trim() === '') {
39.                     const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
40.                     diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
41.                         valueRange,
42.                         `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
43.                         vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
44.                         `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
45.                     ));
46.                 }
47.             }
48.         }
49.     });
50. 
51.     return diagnostics;
52. };
53.
```

</details>

/workspace/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts

<details>
<summary>51 lines</summary>

```
1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
3. import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates that mutually exclusive attributes are not used together on the same tag.
7.  * Uses the `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on TagAttribute definitions.
8.  * Reports each conflict pair only once using alphabetical ordering.
9.  */
10. export const validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
11.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
12.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
13. 
14.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
15.         return diagnostics;
16.     }
17. 
18.     const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
19.     const reportedPairs = new Set<string>();
20. 
21.     tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
22.         if (!attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith || attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith.length === 0) {
23.             return;
24.         }
25. 
26.         const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
27.         if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
28.             return;
29.         }
30. 
31.         for (const conflictName of attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith) {
32.             const conflictNameLower = conflictName.toLowerCase();
33.             if (presentAttrsLower.has(conflictNameLower)) {
34.                 // Use alphabetical ordering to report each conflict only once
35.                 const pairKey = [attrNameLower, conflictNameLower].sort().join('|');
36.                 if (!reportedPairs.has(pairKey)) {
37.                     reportedPairs.add(pairKey);
38.                     diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
39.                         tagInstance.tagRange,
40.                         `Attributes '${attrDef.name}' and '${conflictName}' cannot be used together on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
41.                         vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
42.                         `TAG-EXCLUSIVE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}`
43.                     ));
44.                 }
45.             }
46.         }
47.     });
48. 
49.     return diagnostics;
50. };
51.
```

</details>

1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
3. import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates that attributes with a `dependsOn` requirement have the dependency attribute present.
7.  * For example, code_link's `id` attribute depends on `source` being present.
8.  */
9. export const validateAttributeDependency: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
10.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
11.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
12. 
13.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         return diagnostics;
15.     }
16. 
17.     const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
18. 
19.     tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
20.         if (!attrDef.dependsOn) {
21.             return;
22.         }
23. 
24.         const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
25.         if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
26.             return;
27.         }
28. 
29.         const dependencyLower = attrDef.dependsOn.toLowerCase();
30.         if (!presentAttrsLower.has(dependencyLower)) {
31.             diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
32.                 tagInstance.tagRange,
33.                 `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' requires '${attrDef.dependsOn}' to be present on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
34.                 vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
35.                 `TAG-DEP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
36.             ));
37.         }
38.     });
39. 
40.     return diagnostics;
41. };
42.
```

<details>
<summary>50 lines</summary>

```
1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
3. import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates the value of positional attributes (argv1) against the allowed values list
7.  * defined in the tag's positional attribute definition.
8.  * 
9.  * This complements validateTagAttributeAllowedValues which only checks named attributes.
10.  * Tags like banner have a positional 'variant' with allowed values that need validation.
11.  */
12. export const validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
13.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
14.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
15. 
16.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
17.         return diagnostics;
18.     }
19. 
20.     // Find positional attribute definition with allowed values
21.     const positionalAttrDef = tagDefinition.attributes.find(
22.         attr => attr.isPositional && attr.values && Array.isArray(attr.values) && attr.values.length > 0
23.     );
24. 
25.     if (!positionalAttrDef) {
26.         return diagnostics;
27.     }
28. 
29.     const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
30. 
31.     // Only validate if a positional value is actually present
32.     if (positionalValue === undefined || positionalValue.trim() === '') {
33.         return diagnostics;
34.     }
35. 
36.     const allowedValuesSet = new Set(positionalAttrDef.values!.map(String));
37. 
38.     if (!allowedValuesSet.has(positionalValue)) {
39.         const allowedList = positionalAttrDef.values!.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
40.         diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
41.             tagInstance.tagRange,
42.             `Invalid value '${positionalValue}' for positional attribute '${positionalAttrDef.name}' in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
43.             vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
44.             `TAG-POS-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${positionalAttrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
45.         ));
46.     }
47. 
48.     return diagnostics;
49. };
50.
```

</details>

8. // --- Type Signatures specific to Tag attributes ---
9. export type AttributeValueProviderFn = (
10.   document: vscode.TextDocument,
11.   position: vscode.Position,
12.   tagContext: TagContext, // Uses TagContext
13.   attrDef: TagAttribute, // Use TagAttribute here
14.   currentAttributes: Record<string, string> // Tag attributes are usually strings

16. 
17. /**
18.  * Defines the signature for functions that generate a `vscode.CompletionItem`
19.  * used for inserting a tag as a snippet.
20.  *
21.  * @async
22.  * @returns {Promise<vscode.CompletionItem>} A Promise resolving to the completion item for the snippet.
23.  */
24. export type SnippetProviderFn = () => Promise<vscode.CompletionItem>;
25. 
26. /**
27.  * Defines the signature for functions that generate decoration options for a specific tag instance.
28.  * These functions are typically assigned to the `decorationProvider` property
29.  * of a {@link TagDefinition} definition.
30.  *
31.  * @param {Readonly<TagInstanceInfo>} tagInstance - Information about the specific tag instance found in the document.
32.  * @param {vscode.TextDocument} document - The text document being processed.
33.  * @returns {vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined} An array of decoration options to apply to this tag instance, or undefined if no decorations are needed.
34.  */
35. export type TagDecorationProviderFn = (
36.   tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo,
37.   document: vscode.TextDocument
38. ) => vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined; // Returns an array of decorations for this instance
39. 
40. /**
41.  * Defines the allowed names for decoration types used in TagDecorationProviderConfig.
42.  * This ensures type safety when referencing decoration styles.
43.  * Add new valid decoration type names here.
44.  */
45. export type DecorationTypeName =
46.     | 'codeHighlight' // For code block line highlighting
47.     | 'codeLinkPreview' // For code_link file preview hover
48.     | 'gutterIcon'
49.     | 'relativeLineHint' // For the "(Ln X)" hint shown at the end of the current line in code blocks
50.     | 'warningBlockBorder' // For styling warning admonition blocks <-- NEW
51.     | 'airaInstructionsBlockBorder' // For styling AI assistant instruction blocks
52.     ;
53. 
54. /**
55.  * Configuration object linking a specific decoration provider function
56.  * to a named decoration type. Used within the {@link TagDefinition}'s `decorationProviders` array.
57.  */
58. export interface TagDecorationProviderConfig {
59.   /**
60.    * A unique key identifying the type or style of decoration this provider generates
61.    * (e.g., 'highlight', 'gutterIcon', 'warningBorder'). This key will be used by the
62.    * central handler to retrieve the corresponding vscode.TextEditorDecorationType
63.    * from the DecorationManager.
64.    */
65.   decorationTypeName: DecorationTypeName;
66.   /**
67.    * The function implementing the TagDecorationProviderFn signature, responsible for
68.    * generating the actual DecorationOptions for this specific type and tag instance.
69.    */
70.   provider: TagDecorationProviderFn;
71. }
72. 
73. /**
74.  * Defines the signature for functions that can dynamically provide
75.  * completion items for the *value* of a specific tag attribute.
76.  * These functions are typically assigned to the `loadSupportedValues` property
77.  * of a {@link TagAttribute} definition.
78.  *
79.  * @param document The text document being edited.
80.  * @param position The current cursor position where completion is requested.
81.  * @param tagContext Contextual information about the enclosing tag instance, provided by {@link TagUtils.findEnclosingTagRange}.
82.  * @param attrDef The definition of the specific attribute whose value is being completed, from the {@link TagDefinition}.
83.  * @param currentAttributes A record containing the names and values of attributes already present in the current tag instance, parsed by {@link TagUtils.parseAttributes}.
84.  * @returns A Promise resolving to an array of {@link vscode.CompletionItem} suggestions for the attribute's value, or `undefined`/empty array if no suggestions apply.
85.  */
86. export type AttributeValueProvider = (
87.   document: vscode.TextDocument,
88.   position: vscode.Position,
89.   tagContext: TagContext,
90.   attrDef: TagAttribute,
91.   currentAttributes: Record<string, string>
92. ) => Promise<vscode.CompletionItem[] | undefined>;
93. 
94. /**
95.  * Defines a specific attribute for a Kentico tag.
96.  * Extends BaseAttribute with tag-specific properties.
97.  */
98. export interface TagAttribute extends BaseAttribute { 
99.   /** Is this attribute specified by position rather than name? (e.g., {% image src %}) */
100.   isPositional?: boolean;
101. 
102.   /** Should the snippet completion omit '=value'? (e.g., for boolean flags like 'border') */
103.   omitValueSnippet?: boolean;
104. 
105.   /** Delegate function to load supported values dynamically */
106.   loadSupportedValues?: AttributeValueProviderFn; // Uses the specific Tag signature
107. 
108.   /** 
109.    * Optional delegate function for "Go to Definition" (F12) navigation.
110.    * When the cursor is on this attribute's value and F12 is pressed,
111.    * this function will be called to resolve the definition location.
112.    */
113.   definitionProvider?: AttributeDefinitionProviderFn;
114. 
115.   /**
116.    * Optional list of attribute names that conflict with this attribute.
117.    * If any of the listed attributes are also present on the same tag instance,
118.    * a warning diagnostic will be raised.
119.    */
120.   mutuallyExclusiveWith?: string[];
121. 
122.   /**
123.    * Optional name of another attribute that must be present for this attribute to be valid.
124.    * If this attribute is used but the dependency attribute is missing, a warning is raised.
125.    */
126.   dependsOn?: string;
127. }
128. 
129. /**
130.  * Defines the structure, attributes, and completion behavior for a specific Kentico tag.
131.  */
132. export interface TagDefinition {
133.   /**
134.    * The unique identifier for the tag, using the {@link TagNames} enum.
135.    * Matches the keyword used after '{%'.
136.    */
137.   tagName: TagNames;
138.   /**
139.    * An array defining the possible attributes for this tag, using the {@link TagAttribute} interface.
140.    */
141.   attributes: TagAttribute[];
142.   /**
143.    * Function that generates the `{@link vscode.CompletionItem}`
144.    * used for inserting this tag as a snippet. See {@link SnippetProviderFn}.
145.    * If omitted, no basic snippet completion will be offered for this tag
146.    * by the central snippet provider.
147.    */
148.   snippetProvider: SnippetProviderFn;
149.   /** Optional: Specifies the *only* tag this tag can be a 
150.    * direct child of for snippet suggestions. */
151.   allowedParents?: TagNames[];
152.   /** Set to true if the tag requires a closing {% end... %} 
153.    * tag (e.g., {% code %}{% endcode %}). Defaults to false if omitted. */
154.   isPairTag: boolean;
155.   /**
156.      * Optional array of validation functions specific to this tag definition.
157.      * These rules run *in addition* to any globally registered tag validation rules.
158.      */
159.   validationRules?: TagValidationRuleFn[];
160.   /**
161.     * Optional array of document-level validation functions specific to this tag definition.
162.     * These rules run once per document during the document-level validation phase,
163.     * *in addition* to any globally registered document-level tag validation rules.
164.     * They receive the full list of tags found in the document.
165.     */
166.   documentValidationRules?: TagDocumentValidationRuleFn[];
167. 
168.   /**
169.      * Optional array configuring decoration providers for this tag. Each entry links
170.      * a provider function to a specific named decoration type (style).
171.      * See {@link TagDecorationProviderConfig}.
172.      */
173.   decorationProviders?: TagDecorationProviderConfig[];
174. }
175. 
176. /**
177.  * Represents the context within an opening tag, primarily used for attribute value completions.
178.  */
179. export interface TagContext {
180.   tagContent: string;         // The full text of the tag, e.g., '{% card ... %}'
181.   tagRange: vscode.Range;     // The range of the tag in the document
182.   startTagPos: vscode.Position; // The start position of the tag
183. }
184.

1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagAttribute, TagDefinition } from '../../../definitions/tags/types'; 
3. import { TagNames } from '../../../constants';
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Context passed to each tag validation rule function.
7.  * Encapsulates all necessary information about the tag instance being checked.
8.  */
9. export interface TagValidationContext {
10.     /** The document being validated. */
11.     document: vscode.TextDocument;
12.     // The current editor
13.     editor: vscode.TextEditor;
14.     /** The definition object for the tag type (e.g., card, code). */
15.     tagDefinition: TagDefinition;
16.     /** Information about the specific tag instance found in the document. */
17.     tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo
18.     /** Optional: The TagNames enum value of the immediate enclosing parent tag, if applicable and found. */
19.     parentTagName?: TagNames; // Using the TagNames enum type directly might require importing it if not already via TagDefinition
20.     // Consider adding:
21.     // - Full parsed document structure (if needed by complex rules)?
22.     // - Stack information (if rules need to know about siblings/nesting depth beyond immediate parent)?
23.     /**
24.      * Optional: If the current tagInstance is a closing tag, this property
25.      * holds information about its corresponding opening tag instance found earlier.
26.      * Used for rules that need to compare opening and closing tags (e.g., indentation).
27.      */
28.     matchingOpeningTag?: TagInstanceInfo;
29. }
30. 
31. /**
32.  * Holds key information about a specific tag instance found in the document.
33.  */
34. export interface TagInstanceInfo {
35.     /** The full text of the tag, e.g., '{% card title="x" %}' or '{% endcard %}' */
36.     tagText: string;
37.     /** The range (start and end position) of the tag instance in the document. */
38.     tagRange: vscode.Range;
39.     /** The parsed attributes (key-value pairs) of the opening tag. Empty for closing tags. */
40.     attributes: Record<string, string>;
41.     /** True if this instance is an opening tag, false otherwise. */
42.     isOpeningTag: boolean;
43.      /** True if this instance is a closing tag, false otherwise. */
44.     isClosingTag: boolean;
45.     /** The calculated indentation level (number of leading spaces/tabs) of the line the tag starts on. */
46.     indentation: number;
47.     /** The name of the tag */
48.     tagName: TagNames; // Use TagNames enum type
49. }
50. 
51. /**
52.  * Defines the signature for a function that validates the document globally
53.  * based on the collection of all tags found.
54.  *
55.  * @param document The document being validated.
56.  * @param allTagsInfo An array containing information about every tag instance found in the document.
57.  * @param finalTagStack The final state of the tag stack after processing all tags,
58.  * useful for rules like checking unclosed tags. The stack items contain TagInstanceInfo.
59.  * @param mismatchedClosingTags A list of closing tags that were encountered but did not
60.  * match the expected opening tag on the stack.
61.  * @returns An array of diagnostics found by the rule.
62.  */
63. export type TagDocumentValidationRuleFn = (
64.     document: vscode.TextDocument,
65.     allTagsInfo: Readonly<TagInstanceInfo[]>, // Make input readonly
66.     finalTagStack?: Readonly<TagInstanceInfo[]>, // Make stack readonly and optional
67.     mismatchedClosingTags?: Readonly<TagInstanceInfo[]>
68. ) => vscode.Diagnostic[];
69. 
70. /**
71.  * Defines the signature for a function that validates a specific aspect of a tag.
72.  * Each rule receives the validation context and returns an array of diagnostics.

1. import * as vscode from 'vscode'; // Import vscode for DiagnosticSeverity
2. 
3. import { TagNames } from "../../../../../constants";
4. import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/helpers';
5. import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
6. // --- Define the Tag-Specific Validation Rule ---
7. 
8. export const validateCardIconImageConflict: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
9.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
10.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
11. 
12.     // Check only applies to the 'card' tag's opening instance
13.     if (tagDefinition.tagName === TagNames.CARD && tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         // Case-insensitive check for attribute existence using parsed attributes
15.         const hasImage = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'image');
16.         const hasIcon = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'icon');
17. 
18.         if (hasImage && hasIcon) {
19.              // Try to highlight the whole tag; refining range might be complex here
20.              diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
21.                 tagInstance.tagRange,
22.                 `Card tag should define either 'icon' or 'image', not both.`,
23.                 vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Conflict is a Warning
24.                 `TAG-CARD-CONFLICT` // Unique code for this diagnostic
25.             ));
26.         }
27.     }
28.     return diagnostics;
29. };
```

1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';

3. import { createTagDiagnostic, checkTagAttributeType, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers'; // Adjust import path
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates the data type of existing attributes based on the TagAttribute definition.
7.  */
8. export const validateTagAttributeType: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
9.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
10.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
11. 
12.     // Only check attributes on opening tags
13.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         return diagnostics;
15.     }
16. 
17.     tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
18.         // Check type only if a dataType is defined for the attribute
19.         if (attrDef.dataType) {
20.             const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
21.             // Check if the attribute exists in the parsed instance (case-insensitive keys from parser)
22.             if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
23.                 const actualValueString = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
24.                 // Use the helper function to check the type based on the string value
25.                 if (!checkTagAttributeType(actualValueString, attrDef.dataType)) {
26.                     // Try to find the specific range of the attribute value for better highlighting
27.                     const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange; // Fallback to whole tag range
28. 
29.                     diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
30.                         valueRange,
31.                         `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' has incorrect type. Expected type '${attrDef.dataType}'.`,
32.                         vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Type mismatches are often Warnings
33.                         `TAG-TYPE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
34.                     ));
35.                 }
36.             }
37.         }
38.     });
39. 
40.     return diagnostics;
41. };
42.

/workspace/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateTagAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts

<details>
<summary>46 lines</summary>

```
1. import * as vscode from 'vscode';
2. import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types'; // Adjust import path
3. import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers'; // Adjust import path
4. 
5. /**
6.  * Validates attribute values against a predefined list in the TagAttribute definition, if present.
7.  */
8. export const validateTagAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
9.     const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
10.     const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
11. 
12.     // Only check attributes on opening tags
13.     if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
14.         return diagnostics;
15.     }
16. 
17.      tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
18.          // Check only if a static list of values is defined and the attribute exists
19.          if (attrDef.values && Array.isArray(attrDef.values) && attrDef.values.length > 0) {
20.              const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
21.              // Check if the attribute exists in the parsed instance (case-insensitive keys from parser)
22.              if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
23.                  const actualValue = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
24.                  // Convert allowed values to strings for comparison as parsed attributes are strings
25.                  const allowedValuesSet = new Set(attrDef.values.map(String));
26. 
27.                  // Check if the actual value is in the allowed set
28.                  if (!allowedValuesSet.has(actualValue)) {
29.                       // Try to find the specific range of the attribute value
30.                       const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange; // Fallback
31.                       const allowedList = attrDef.values.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', '); // Format for message
32. 
33.                       diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
34.                           valueRange,
35.                           `Invalid value '${actualValue}' for attribute '${attrDef.name}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
36.                           vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Using a wrong value is typically a Warning
37.                           `TAG-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
38.                       ));
39.                  }
40.              }
41.          }
42.      });
43. 
44.     return diagnostics;
45. };
46.
```

</details>

## Scout Report — DOC-3141

### PR
- PR ID: 2901
- PR URL: https://dev.azure.com/anthropic/kentico-docs/_git/kentico-docs-autocomplete-vscode/pullrequest/2901
- Title: DOC-3141 - Add generic tag attribute validation rules
- Branch: `ralph/doc-3141` → `main`
- Author: Jakub Skurek
- Status: Active (not draft)

### Summary
This PR adds 5 new generic tag attribute validation rules (applied automatically to all tags via `rulesRegister.ts`), extends `TagAttribute` with two new declarative properties (`mutuallyExclusiveWith`, `dependsOn`), adds `dataType` to all attributes across all 27 tag definitions, replaces one custom per-tag validation rule with the generic declarative approach, and includes comprehensive tests (340 lines).

This was a latent type mismatch bug — the `values` field type is `(string | number | boolean)[]` so it compiled, but the validation rule comparison would have failed since `false !== 'false'`.

3. **cell colspan/rowspan dataType fix**: Changed from `AttributeDataType.String` to `AttributeDataType.Number`. The `values` arrays (`[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`) are number literals, so `Number` is correct.

6. **Duplicate attribute detection uses regex on raw tag text**: The `validateDuplicateAttributes` rule re-parses the raw tag text rather than relying on `tagInstance.attributes` (which is a dictionary and would lose duplicates). This is the correct approach.

7. **Import of `TagValidationContext` in some rules**: Both `validateDuplicateAttributes` and `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` import `TagValidationContext` but only use `TagValidationRuleFn` from the types. The `context` parameter is typed via `TagValidationRuleFn`, so the `TagValidationContext` import is unused. However, lint passes with `--max-warnings 0`, so TypeScript/ESLint are apparently not flagging this (possibly the type is used for destructuring inference).

# Copilot Instructions - Kentico Documentation Markdown Completions

VS Code extension providing autocomplete, diagnostics, and decorations for Kentico-flavored Markdown (KFM).

## Architecture Overview

This extension follows a **definition-driven architecture** where tag/attribute behaviors are declared in definition files, then consumed by centralized providers:

```
src/
├── definitions/         # Declarative definitions (tags, headers, YAML)
│   ├── tags/           # Tag definitions by category (block, inline, links, etc.)
│   ├── header/         # YAML frontmatter attribute definitions
│   └── yaml/           # YAML file completions for config files
├── logic/              # Runtime logic consuming definitions
│   ├── completions/    # Completion providers (snippets, attributes, values)
│   ├── diagnostics/    # Validation rules and diagnostic handlers
│   ├── decorations/    # Editor decorations (highlights, gutters)
│   ├── events/         # Internal event emitter system
│   └── _helpers/       # Tag parsing utilities (TagUtils, tagScanner)
└── constants.ts        # TagNames enum, regex patterns, symbols
```

## Adding a New Tag

1. **Add to `TagNames` enum** in `src/constants.ts`
2. **Create definition folder** under `src/definitions/tags/{category}/{tagName}/`
3. **Create `{tagName}.types.ts`** implementing `TagDefinition` interface:
   ```typescript
   export const myTagDefinition: TagDefinition = {
       tagName: TagNames.MY_TAG,
       isPairTag: true,  // requires {% endmy_tag %}
       attributes: [...],
       snippetProvider: myTagSnippet,
       validationRules: [...],  // optional per-instance rules
       decorationProviders: [...],  // optional decorations
   };
   ```
4. **Register in `definitionInit.ts`** - import and add to `definitionsToRegister` array

## Adding a Header Attribute

Header attributes handle YAML frontmatter completions/diagnostics (title, license, related_pages, etc.):

1. **Create folder** under `src/definitions/header/{attributeName}/`
2. **Create `attributeDefinition.ts`** implementing `HeaderAttribute`:
   ```typescript
   export const myAttributeDefinition: HeaderAttribute = {
       name: 'my_attribute',
       required: false,
       description: 'Description for hover',
       dataType: AttributeDataType.String,
       loadSupportedValues: myDataProvider,  // optional dynamic values
       getMissingAttributeCompletion: myMissingProvider,  // optional custom snippet
       validationRules: [...],  // optional
   };
   ```
3. **Register in `headerDefinition.ts`** - add to `markdownHeaderDefinition.attributes[]`

For dynamic values, create a `*DataProvider.ts` that loads from workspace files (see `licenseDataProvider.ts`).

## Decoration System

Decorations provide visual feedback in the editor (highlights, gutter icons, hints):

1. **Add decoration type name** to `DecorationTypeName` union in `src/definitions/tags/types.ts`
2. **Register decoration style** in `src/logic/decorations/decorationTypeManager.ts` - define the `vscode.TextEditorDecorationType`
3. **Create provider function** implementing `TagDecorationProviderFn`:
   ```typescript
   export function myDecorations(
       tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo,
       document: vscode.TextDocument
   ): vscode.DecorationOptions[] | undefined {
       // Return decoration options for ranges within this tag
   }
   ```
4. **Add to tag's `decorationProviders[]`** array linking `decorationTypeName` to `provider`

Example: `src/definitions/tags/block/code/codeDecorations.ts` provides line highlighting for `{% code %}` blocks.

## Key Patterns

### Definition Registry
All tag definitions register via `registerTagDefinition()` in `src/definitions/definitionRegister.ts`. Access definitions with `getTagDefinition(TagNames.X)` or `getAllTagDefinitions()`.

### Attribute Value Providers
Dynamic completions use `loadSupportedValues` functions - see `src/definitions/header/license/licenseDataProvider.ts` for loading from workspace files.

### Validation Rules
- **Per-instance**: `TagValidationRuleFn` in tag's `validationRules[]`
- **Document-level**: `TagDocumentValidationRuleFn` in `documentValidationRules[]`
- **Generic rules** in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/` — applied to all tags automatically:
  - `validateRequiredTagAttribute` — missing required attributes
  - `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` — required attributes present but empty
  - `validateTagAttributeAllowedValues` — named attribute values against allowed list
  - `validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues` — positional (argv1) values against allowed list
  - `validateDuplicateAttributes` — same attribute name used multiple times
  - `validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes` — conflicting attributes used together (via `mutuallyExclusiveWith`)
  - `validateAttributeDependency` — attribute used without its required dependency (via `dependsOn`)
  - `validateTagAttributeType` — attribute value type checking
  - `validateUnknownTagAttribute` — unrecognized attribute names
  - `validateAllowedParent` — parent tag context
  - `validatePairTagIndentation` — opening/closing tag indentation match
  - `validateNewlineAfterListBeforeEndTag` — list before closing tag
- Rules return `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
- Register new generic rules in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`

### Internal Events
Event-driven updates via `src/logic/events/internalEventEmitter.ts`:
- `activationComplete`, `editorChanged`, `documentChanged`, `selectionChanged`, `configChanged`
- Subscribe in `src/logic/eventSubscriber.ts`

### Tag Parsing
Use `TagUtils` from `src/logic/_helpers/tagUtils.ts`:
- `findEnclosingTagRange()` - get tag context at cursor
- `parseAttributes()` - extract named/positional attrs
- `isInsideOpeningTagDefinition()` - check cursor location

## code_link Tag Implementation

The `{% code_link %}` tag is a complex feature with multiple providers. Reference implementation in `src/definitions/tags/block/codeLink/`:

| File | Purpose |
|------|---------|
| `codeLink.types.ts` | Tag definition with attributes (source, id, exclude, lang, etc.) |
| `codeLinkService.ts` | Shared utilities (path resolution, section extraction, validation) |
| `codeLinkValidation.ts` | Validation rules for source file and section markers |
| `codeLinkIdDataProvider.ts` | Autocomplete for `id`/`exclude` attrs (scans source for `//Include:` markers) |
| `codeLinkSourceDataProvider.ts` | Autocomplete for `source` attr (scans code_link_include_root) |
| `codeLinkCodeLens.ts` | CodeLens in markdown showing file preview on hover |
| `codeLinkSourceCodeLens.ts` | CodeLens in source files showing reference counts |
| `codeLinkReferenceScanner.ts` | Scans documentation folders for code_link usage |
| `codeLinkDefinitionProvider.ts` | F12 "Go to Definition" for source attribute |
| `codeLinkDecorations.ts` | Editor decorations for code_link tags |

### CodeLens Registration
CodeLens providers are registered via `src/logic/codelens/codeLensRegister.ts` which is called from `pluginInit.ts`.

## Testing

Tests are in `src/test/` using Mocha. Run with `npm run test:xvfb` (Linux) or `npm test`.

- Tests use pure utility functions from `codeLinkTestUtils.ts` (no VS Code API dependency)
- Test files organized by feature: `validation.test.ts`, `highlight.test.ts`, `referenceScanner.test.ts`, etc.
- First-time Linux setup: `npm run test:setup` installs xvfb dependencies

## Development Commands

```bash
npm run watch      # Compile with webpack in watch mode
npm run lint       # ESLint
npm run test       # Run tests (requires compile-tests first)
npm run test:xvfb  # Run tests in headless mode (Linux)
npm run build      # Package VSIX
```

**Debug**: Press F5 to launch Extension Development Host. Extension activates when workspace contains `**/src/_configs/_config_primary.yml`.

## Key Files for Context

- Tag syntax patterns: `src/constants.ts` (`CODE_START_REGEX`, `SYMBOLS`)
- Workspace file loading: `src/logic/filesystem/workspaceFileLoader.ts`
- Grammar injection: `grammars/injections/kfmarkdown.json`
<exited with exit code 0>
````

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e9885ad
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateAttributeDependency.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,41 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that attributes with a `dependsOn` requirement have the dependency attribute present.
+ * For example, code_link's `id` attribute depends on `source` being present.
+ */
+export const validateAttributeDependency: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.dependsOn) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const dependencyLower = attrDef.dependsOn.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(dependencyLower)) {
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${attrDef.name}' requires '${attrDef.dependsOn}' to be present on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DEP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..37800f9
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,53 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
+ * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
+ */
+export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;
+
+    // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
+    const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
+    if (!tagNameMatch) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
+    // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
+    const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
+    const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count
+
+    let match;
+    while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
+        const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
+        seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
+    }
+
+    for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
+        if (count > 1) {
+            // Find the original casing from the tag text
+            const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
+            const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
+            const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;
+
+            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                tagInstance.tagRange,
+                `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
+                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
+            ));
+        }
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..4d5bc70
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,52 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
+ * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
+ */
+export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.required) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        if (attrDef.isPositional) {
+            // Check positional argv1 for empty value
+            const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+            if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+                diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                    tagInstance.tagRange,
+                    `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                    vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                    `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                ));
+            }
+        } else {
+            // Check named required attributes for empty values
+            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
+                const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
+                if (value.trim() === '') {
+                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        valueRange,
+                        `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
+                        `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..3c6818d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,50 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates that mutually exclusive attributes are not used together on the same tag.
+ * Uses the `mutuallyExclusiveWith` property on TagAttribute definitions.
+ * Reports each conflict pair only once using alphabetical ordering.
+ */
+export const validateMutuallyExclusiveAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const presentAttrsLower = new Set(Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).map(k => k.toLowerCase()));
+    const reportedPairs = new Set<string>();
+
+    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
+        if (!attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith || attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith.length === 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const attrNameLower = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
+        if (!presentAttrsLower.has(attrNameLower)) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        for (const conflictName of attrDef.mutuallyExclusiveWith) {
+            const conflictNameLower = conflictName.toLowerCase();
+            if (presentAttrsLower.has(conflictNameLower)) {
+                // Use alphabetical ordering to report each conflict only once
+                const pairKey = [attrNameLower, conflictNameLower].sort().join('|');
+                if (!reportedPairs.has(pairKey)) {
+                    reportedPairs.add(pairKey);
+                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+                        tagInstance.tagRange,
+                        `Attributes '${attrDef.name}' and '${conflictName}' cannot be used together on '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
+                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+                        `TAG-EXCLUSIVE-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}`
+                    ));
+                }
+            }
+        }
+    });
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};
diff --git a/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..396feca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues.rule.ts
@@ -0,0 +1,49 @@
+import * as vscode from 'vscode';
+import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
+import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../helpers';
+
+/**
+ * Validates the value of positional attributes (argv1) against the allowed values list
+ * defined in the tag's positional attribute definition.
+ * 
+ * This complements validateTagAttributeAllowedValues which only checks named attributes.
+ * Tags like banner have a positional 'variant' with allowed values that need validation.
+ */
+export const validatePositionalAttributeAllowedValues: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
+    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
+    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];
+
+    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    // Find positional attribute definition with allowed values
+    const positionalAttrDef = tagDefinition.attributes.find(
+        attr => attr.isPositional && attr.values && Array.isArray(attr.values) && attr.values.length > 0
+    );
+
+    if (!positionalAttrDef) {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
+
+    // Only validate if a positional value is actually present
+    if (positionalValue === undefined || positionalValue.trim() === '') {
+        return diagnostics;
+    }
+
+    const allowedValuesSet = new Set(positionalAttrDef.values!.map(String));
+
+    if (!allowedValuesSet.has(positionalValue)) {
+        const allowedList = positionalAttrDef.values!.map(v => `'${v}'`).join(', ');
+        diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
+            tagInstance.tagRange,
+            `Invalid value '${positionalValue}' for positional attribute '${positionalAttrDef.name}' in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Allowed values are: ${allowedList}.`,
+            vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
+            `TAG-POS-VAL-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${positionalAttrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
+        ));
+    }
+
+    return diagnostics;
+};

+import * as assert from 'assert';

+// Helper to create a test document and build a validation context
+async function buildContext(
+    tagText: string,
+    tagDefinition: TagDefinition,
+    options?: { isClosingTag?: boolean }
+): Promise<TagValidationContext> {
+    const document = await vscode.workspace.openTextDocument({ content: tagText, language: 'markdown' });
+    const isClosing = options?.isClosingTag ?? false;
+    const tagRange = new vscode.Range(
+        new vscode.Position(0, 0),
+        new vscode.Position(0, tagText.length)
+    );
+
+    const attributes = isClosing ? {} : TagUtils.parseAttributes(tagText);
+
+    const tagInstance: TagInstanceInfo = {
+        tagText,
+        tagRange,
+        attributes,
+        isOpeningTag: !isClosing,
+        isClosingTag: isClosing,
+        indentation: 0,
+        tagName: tagDefinition.tagName
+    };
+
+    return {
+        document,
+        editor: {} as vscode.TextEditor, // Not used by these rules
+        tagDefinition,
+        tagInstance
+    };
+}
+
+// Minimal snippet provider for test definitions
