**Full duplicate attrs rule**

$ cat src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts

<details>
<summary>45 lines</summary>

```
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeNameRange } from '../helpers';

/**
 * Validates that no attribute name appears more than once in a tag instance.
 * Scans the raw tag text for duplicate named attribute keys.
 */
export const validateDuplicateAttributes: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        return diagnostics;
    }

    const tagText = tagInstance.tagText;

    // Extract the content after the tag name and before closing %}
    const tagNameMatch = tagText.match(/^\{%\s*(\w+)\s*/);
    if (!tagNameMatch) {
        return diagnostics;
    }

    const afterTagName = tagText.substring(tagNameMatch[0].length);
    // Find all named attribute keys (word=...) in the remaining text
    const namedAttrRegex = /\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)/gi;
    const seenAttributes = new Map<string, number>(); // lowercase name -> count

    let match;
    while ((match = namedAttrRegex.exec(afterTagName)) !== null) {
        const attrName = match[1].toLowerCase();
        seenAttributes.set(attrName, (seenAttributes.get(attrName) || 0) + 1);
    }

    for (const [attrName, count] of seenAttributes) {
        if (count > 1) {
            // Find the original casing from the tag text
            const casingRegex = new RegExp(`\\b(${attrName})\\b`, 'i');
            const casingMatch = tagText.match(casingRegex);
            const originalCasing = casingMatch?.[1] || attrName;

            diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                tagInstance.tagRange,
                `Attribute '${originalCasing}' is specified ${count} times in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'. Each attribute should only appear once.`,
                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning,
                `TAG-DUP-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrName.toUpperCase()}`
            ));
        }
    }

    return diagnostics;
};
<exited with exit code 0>
```

<details>
<summary>48 lines</summary>

```
import * as vscode from 'vscode';
import { TagValidationContext, TagValidationRuleFn } from '../types';
import { createTagDiagnostic, findTagAttributeValueRange } from '../helpers';

/**
 * Validates that required attributes, when present, do not have empty values.
 * This catches cases like title="" or src="" where the attribute exists but is blank.
 */
export const validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance, document } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        return diagnostics;
    }

    tagDefinition.attributes.forEach(attrDef => {
        if (!attrDef.required) {
            return;
        }

        if (attrDef.isPositional) {
            // Check positional argv1 for empty value
            const positionalValue = tagInstance.attributes['argv1'];
            if (positionalValue !== undefined && positionalValue.trim() === '') {
                diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                    tagInstance.tagRange,
                    `Required ${attrDef.name} has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
                    vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
                    `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
                ));
            }
        } else {
            // Check named required attributes for empty values
            const lowerAttrName = attrDef.name.toLowerCase();
            if (tagInstance.attributes.hasOwnProperty(lowerAttrName)) {
                const value = tagInstance.attributes[lowerAttrName];
                if (value.trim() === '') {
                    const valueRange = findTagAttributeValueRange(document, tagInstance.tagRange, attrDef.name) || tagInstance.tagRange;
                    diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                        valueRange,
                        `Required attribute '${attrDef.name}' has an empty value in tag '{% ${tagDefinition.tagName} %}'.`,
                        vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Error,
                        `TAG-EMPTY-${tagDefinition.tagName.toUpperCase()}-${attrDef.name.toUpperCase()}`
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    });

    return diagnostics;
};
<exited with exit code 0>
```

import * as vscode from 'vscode'; // Import vscode for DiagnosticSeverity

import { TagNames } from "../../../../../constants";
import { createTagDiagnostic } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/helpers';
import { TagValidationRuleFn } from '../../../../../logic/diagnostics/tags/types';
// --- Define the Tag-Specific Validation Rule ---

export const validateCardIconImageConflict: TagValidationRuleFn = (context) => {
    const { tagDefinition, tagInstance } = context;
    const diagnostics: vscode.Diagnostic[] = [];

    // Check only applies to the 'card' tag's opening instance
    if (tagDefinition.tagName === TagNames.CARD && tagInstance.isOpeningTag) {
        // Case-insensitive check for attribute existence using parsed attributes
        const hasImage = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'image');
        const hasIcon = Object.keys(tagInstance.attributes).some(attr => attr.toLowerCase() === 'icon');

        if (hasImage && hasIcon) {
             // Try to highlight the whole tag; refining range might be complex here
             diagnostics.push(createTagDiagnostic(
                tagInstance.tagRange,
                `Card tag should define either 'icon' or 'image', not both.`,
                vscode.DiagnosticSeverity.Warning, // Conflict is a Warning
                `TAG-CARD-CONFLICT` // Unique code for this diagnostic
            ));
        }
    }
    return diagnostics;
};

1. ## Scout Report — DOC-3141
2. 
3. ### PR
4. - PR ID: 2901
5. - PR URL: https://dev.azure.com/anthropic/kentico-docs/_git/kentico-docs-autocomplete-vscode/pullrequest/2901
6. - Title: DOC-3141 - Add generic tag attribute validation rules
7. - Branch: `ralph/doc-3141` → `main`
8. - Author: Jakub Skurek
9. - Status: Active (not draft)
10. 
11. ### Summary
12. This PR adds 5 new generic tag attribute validation rules (applied automatically to all tags via `rulesRegister.ts`), extends `TagAttribute` with two new declarative properties (`mutuallyExclusiveWith`, `dependsOn`), adds `dataType` to all attributes across all 27 tag definitions, replaces one custom per-tag validation rule with the generic declarative approach, and includes comprehensive tests (340 lines).
13. 
14. ### Changed Files (39 total — 767 insertions, 46 deletions)
15. 
16. | File | Status | Category | Pattern | Notes |
17. |---|---|---|---|---|
18. | `.github/copilot-instructions.md` | M | Documentation | Agent instructions | Documents all generic rules, adds registration guidance |
19. | `CHANGELOG.md` | M | Documentation | Changelog | Version 1.2.8 entry with all changes |
20. | `kfm-mdcompletions-1.2.8.vsix` | A | Build artifact | VSIX bundle | Pre-built extension package |
21. | `package.json` | M | Config | Version bump | 1.2.7 → 1.2.8 |
22. | `src/definitions/tags/types.ts` | M | Core types | TagAttribute interface | Added `mutuallyExclusiveWith?: string[]` and `dependsOn?: string` |
23. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/info/info.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: Boolean` to icon attr |
24. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/key/key.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: Boolean` to icon attr |
25. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/license_info/licenseInfo.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: String` to env, `Boolean` to icon |
26. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/note/note.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: Boolean` to icon attr |
27. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/tip/tip.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: Boolean` to icon attr |
28. | `src/definitions/tags/admonitions/warning/warning.types.ts` | M | Tag definition | dataType addition | Added `dataType: Boolean` to icon attr |

58. ### Pattern Checklist
59. 
60. - [x] **TagAttribute interface extended**: `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn` added to `src/definitions/tags/types.ts` (lines 114-124)
61. - [x] **Rules registered**: All 5 new rules imported and added to `allTagValidationRules[]` in `src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`
62. - [x] **Rules follow `TagValidationRuleFn` signature**: Each accepts `TagValidationContext`, returns `vscode.Diagnostic[]`
63. - [x] **Rules use `createTagDiagnostic` helper**: All rules use the standard helper from `../helpers`
64. - [x] **Rules skip closing tags**: All 5 rules have `if (!tagInstance.isOpeningTag) return diagnostics;` guard
65. - [x] **Custom rule migrated to declarative**: Card's `validateCardIconImageConflict` replaced by `mutuallyExclusiveWith` on icon/image attributes
66. - [x] **dataType added to all tag definitions**: All 27 tag definition files updated with appropriate `AttributeDataType` values
67. - [x] **Version bumped**: 1.2.7 → 1.2.8 in `package.json`
68. - [x] **CHANGELOG updated**: Full 1.2.8 entry with all changes documented
69. - [x] **Copilot instructions updated**: Documents all generic rules and registration pattern
70. - [N/A] **TagNames enum**: No new tags — N/A
71. - [N/A] **Grammar updated**: No grammar changes — N/A
72. - [N/A] **Disposal**: No new disposables — N/A
73. 
74. ### Build Status
75. - **Compile**: ✅ PASS — webpack compiled successfully in 5247ms, no errors
76. - **Lint**: ✅ PASS — ESLint with `--max-warnings 0` passed (TypeScript version warning is cosmetic, not a lint failure)
77. - **Tests**: ✅ PASS — **452 passing** (861ms), 0 failing
78. 
79. ### Notable Observations
80. 
81. 1. **Old custom rule file not deleted**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` still exists on disk and exports `validateCardIconImageConflict`. The import was removed from `card.types.ts` so it's dead code. The file is not in the diff (not deleted). This is harmless (lint passes, no unused import warnings since the file itself isn't imported anywhere) but is orphaned code.
82. 
83. 2. **pageLink `suppress_warnings` bugfix**: The `values` array had an unquoted `false` boolean literal (`values: ['true', false]`) which was corrected to `values: ['true', 'false']`. This was a latent type mismatch bug — the `values` field type is `(string | number | boolean)[]` so it compiled, but the validation rule comparison would have failed since `false !== 'false'`.
84. 
85. 3. **cell colspan/rowspan dataType fix**: Changed from `AttributeDataType.String` to `AttributeDataType.Number`. The `values` arrays (`[1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]`) are number literals, so `Number` is correct.
86. 
87. 4. **gridItem `allowedParents`**: Added `allowedParents: [TagNames.GRID]` — this is a correctness improvement (grid_item should only appear inside grid).
88. 
89. 5. **codeLink retains `validationRules`**: The codeLink tag still has per-tag `validationRules` for source/section/exclude existence checking. These are content-aware rules that can't be generalized, so this is correct.
90. 
91. 6. **Duplicate attribute detection uses regex on raw tag text**: The `validateDuplicateAttributes` rule re-parses the raw tag text rather than relying on `tagInstance.attributes` (which is a dictionary and would lose duplicates). This is the correct approach.
92. 
93. 7. **Import of `TagValidationContext` in some rules**: Both `validateDuplicateAttributes` and `validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue` import `TagValidationContext` but only use `TagValidationRuleFn` from the types. The `context` parameter is typed via `TagValidationRuleFn`, so the `TagValidationContext` import is unused. However, lint passes with `--max-warnings 0`, so TypeScript/ESLint are apparently not flagging this (possibly the type is used for destructuring inference).
94. 
95. ### Missing Connections
96. 
97. 1. **Orphaned file**: `src/definitions/tags/block/card/diagnostics/imageAttrConflict.ts` is no longer imported anywhere but was not deleted. No functional impact but is dead code.
98. 
99. ### Focus Areas for Reviewers
100. 
101. 1. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateDuplicateAttributes.rule.ts`** — Most complex of the 5 new rules. Uses regex-based parsing of raw tag text to find duplicates. Verify the regex `\b([\w-]+)\s*(?=\s*=)` correctly handles all attribute name patterns and edge cases (e.g., attribute values that contain `=`).
102. 
103. 2. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rules/validateEmptyRequiredAttributeValue.rule.ts`** — Handles both positional (argv1) and named required attributes. Verify the empty-value check logic for positional attributes and whether `undefined` vs empty string is handled correctly at all call sites.
104. 
105. 3. **`src/definitions/tags/types.ts`** — Core interface change adding `mutuallyExclusiveWith` and `dependsOn`. Verify these properties are sufficient for the use cases and whether the types (string[] vs string) are correct for future expansion.
106. 
107. 4. **`src/definitions/tags/block/card/card.types.ts`** — Migration from custom `validateCardIconImageConflict` to declarative `mutuallyExclusiveWith`. Verify the diagnostic messages/codes remain consistent or are intentionally changed.
108. 
109. 5. **`src/definitions/tags/links/pageLink/pageLink.types.ts`** — Contains the `values: ['true', false]` → `['true', 'false']` bugfix. Verify this is the correct fix and no other tag definitions have similar unquoted boolean values.
110. 
111. 6. **`src/definitions/tags/tables/cell/cell.types.ts`** — `colspan`/`rowspan` dataType changed from `String` to `Number`. Verify the existing `validateTagAttributeType` rule handles this correctly with the `values: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6]` number array.
112. 
113. 7. **`src/logic/diagnostics/tags/rulesRegister.ts`** — Verify rule ordering is intentional. The new rules are inserted between existing rules.
114. 
115. 8. **`src/test/diagnostics/tagAttributeValidation.test.ts`** — 340 lines of tests for all 5 rules. Verify edge case coverage is sufficient (e.g., case sensitivity, whitespace, multiple diagnostics on the same tag).
